print("[TURNsaken] Startup...")
task.wait(1)

--==================================================================
-- TURNsaken V3 :: Live Localization + White Liquid-Glass License Gate
--==================================================================
TURNsakenI18n = (function()
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local I18N = {
        Languages = {zh = true, en = true, both = true},
        lang = "zh",
    }

    local bindings = {}
    local hooks = {}

    local function hasCJK(text)
        return type(text) == "string" and text:find("[\228-\233]") ~= nil
    end

    local function tidy(text)
        if type(text) ~= "string" then return text end
        text = text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        text = text:gsub("%s+([:;,.!%?%)%]])", "%1")
        text = text:gsub("%s+", " ")
        return text
    end

    -- Strings that cannot be split safely from the bilingual source text.
    local explicit = {
        ["Mode: Move + Face Killer (移动 + 对准杀手)"] = {
            en = "Mode: Move + Face Killer",
            zh = "模式: 移动 + 对准杀手",
        },
        ["Device Spoof: PC (设备伪装)"] = {
            en = "Device Spoof: PC",
            zh = "设备伪装: PC",
        },
        ["Auto Block\nON"] = {en = "Auto Block\nON", zh = "自动格挡\n开"},
        ["Auto Block\nOFF"] = {en = "Auto Block\nOFF", zh = "自动格挡\n关"},
        ["电影光影已开启 (暖色柔和)"] = {
            en = "Cinematic lighting enabled (warm & soft)",
            zh = "电影光影已开启 (暖色柔和)",
        },
        ["电影光影已关闭"] = {
            en = "Cinematic lighting disabled",
            zh = "电影光影已关闭",
        },
        ["Discord link copied (链接已复制)"] = {
            en = "Discord link copied",
            zh = "Discord 链接已复制",
        },
        ["Tip: 缩放与分辨率自适应叠加，适合手机或小窗口"] = {
            en = "Tip: zoom and resolution fitting stack together - great for phones and small windows.",
            zh = "提示: 缩放与分辨率自适应叠加，适合手机或小窗口",
        },
        ["Tip: 平滑转头 数值越大转得越快 (推荐 5-10)"] = {
            en = "Tip: higher smooth-turn speed turns faster (5-10 recommended)",
            zh = "提示: 平滑转头数值越大转得越快 (推荐 5-10)",
        },
        ["Tip: 数值越小画面越扁 (推荐 75-90)"] = {
            en = "Tip: lower value squashes the image more (75-90 recommended)",
            zh = "提示: 数值越小画面越扁 (推荐 75-90)",
        },
        ["Tip: 选择已保存配置后可直接读取/删除；自动保存会写入当前配置"] = {
            en = "Tip: pick a saved config to load or delete it; Auto Save writes to the current config.",
            zh = "提示: 选择已保存配置后可直接读取/删除；自动保存会写入当前配置",
        },
        ["Tip: 默认 RightShift，可在上方下拉更换 (F15 = disabled)"] = {
            en = "Tip: default is RightShift. Change it in the dropdown above (F15 = disabled).",
            zh = "提示: 默认 RightShift，可在上方下拉更换 (F15 = disabled)",
        },
        ["Tip: 切换语言后立即生效，并会随配置自动保存"] = {
            en = "Tip: the language applies instantly and is saved with your config.",
            zh = "提示: 切换语言后立即生效，并会随配置自动保存",
        },
        ["延迟越大越容易错过服务器判定，推荐 0 ~ 0.15"] = {
            en = "Higher delay makes you miss server checks. 0 ~ 0.15 recommended.",
            zh = "延迟越大越容易错过服务器判定，推荐 0 ~ 0.15",
        },
        ["支持 Flow / Gear / Lock / Connect 四种小游戏"] = {
            en = "Supports all four minigames: Flow / Gear / Lock / Connect",
            zh = "支持 Flow / Gear / Lock / Connect 四种小游戏",
        },
        ["自动拾取 Medkit / BloxyCola / Gear"] = {
            en = "Auto-pickup Medkit / BloxyCola / Gear",
            zh = "自动拾取 Medkit / BloxyCola / Gear",
        },
        ["Zoom (缩放): %d%%  |  Fit (实际): %d%%"] = {
            en = "Zoom: %d%%  |  Fit: %d%%",
            zh = "缩放: %d%%  |  实际: %d%%",
        },
    }

    -- English strings that have no Chinese half.
    local zhExtra = {
        ["TURNsaken Auto Block Script"] = "TURNsaken 自动格挡脚本",
        ["TURNsaken"] = "TURNsaken",
        ["Config"] = "配置",
        ["Scene"] = "场景",
        ["HDT"] = "碰撞箱拖拽",
        ["Loaded"] = "已加载",
        ["Notification"] = "通知",
        ["Config not found"] = "未找到配置",
        ["FPS: -- | PING: -- ms"] = "帧率: -- | 延迟: -- ms",
    }

    -- Chinese-only strings that have no English half.
    local enExtra = {
        ["电影光影已关闭"] = "Cinematic lighting disabled",
    }

    local function splitBilingual(raw)
        local init = 1
        while true do
            local first, last, inner = raw:find("%(([^()]*)[%)]", init)
            if not first then break end
            if hasCJK(inner) then
                local prefix = raw:sub(1, first - 1)
                local suffix = raw:sub(last + 1)
                local en = tidy(prefix .. suffix)
                local zh
                if hasCJK(prefix) then
                    zh = tidy(raw)
                else
                    zh = tidy(inner .. suffix)
                end
                return en, zh
            end
            init = last + 1
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    function I18N.GetLanguage()
        return I18N.lang
    end

    function I18N.Translate(raw)
        if type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" then return raw end
        local lang = I18N.lang
        local ex = explicit[raw]
        if ex then
            if lang == "both" and ex.both then return ex.both end
            if ex[lang] then return ex[lang] end
        end
        if lang == "both" then return raw end
        local en, zh = splitBilingual(raw)
        if lang == "en" then
            if en and en ~= "" then return en end
            if enExtra[raw] then return enExtra[raw] end
            return raw
        end
        if zh and zh ~= "" then return zh end
        if zhExtra[raw] then return zhExtra[raw] end
        return raw
    end

    function I18N.Choose(english, chinese)
        if I18N.lang == "en" then return english end
        if I18N.lang == "both" then return tostring(english) .. " / " .. tostring(chinese) end
        return chinese
    end

    local function applyBinding(entry)
        if not entry or not entry.obj or entry.obj.Parent == nil then return end
        local value = I18N.Translate(entry.raw)
        if entry.mode == "placeholder" then
            entry.obj.PlaceholderText = tostring(entry.prefix or "") .. tostring(value)
        else
            entry.obj.Text = tostring(entry.prefix or "") .. tostring(value)
        end
    end

    function I18N.Bind(obj, raw, opts)
        if not obj or type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" then return nil end
        opts = opts or {}
        local entry = {
            obj = obj,
            raw = raw,
            prefix = opts.prefix or "",
            mode = opts.mode or "text",
        }
        table.insert(bindings, entry)
        pcall(applyBinding, entry)
        return entry
    end

    function I18N.AddHook(fn)
        if type(fn) == "function" then table.insert(hooks, fn) end
    end

    function I18N.Apply(code)
        if not I18N.Languages[code] then code = "zh" end
        I18N.lang = code
        for index = #bindings, 1, -1 do
            local entry = bindings[index]
            if not entry.obj or entry.obj.Parent == nil then
                table.remove(bindings, index)
            else
                pcall(applyBinding, entry)
            end
        end
        for _, fn in ipairs(hooks) do pcall(fn) end
        return code
    end

    -- Notifications contain runtime values ("Saved: Default"), so translate
    -- their stable prefixes after the normal label pass.
    function I18N.NotifyText(text)
        if type(text) ~= "string" then return text end
        local translated = I18N.Translate(text)
        if translated ~= text then return translated end
        if I18N.lang == "en" or I18N.lang == "both" then return translated end
        local value = text:match("^Saved:%s*(.*)$")
        if value then return "已保存: " .. value end
        value = text:match("^Loaded:%s*(.*)$")
        if value then return "已加载: " .. value end
        value = text:match("^Deleted:%s*(.*)$")
        if value then return "已删除: " .. value end
        value = text:match("^Found%s+(%d+)%s+config")
        if value then return "找到 " .. value .. " 个配置" end
        return translated
    end

    function I18N.ZoomText(zoom, fit)
        if I18N.lang == "en" then
            return string.format("Zoom: %d%%  |  Fit: %d%%", zoom, fit)
        elseif I18N.lang == "both" then
            return string.format("缩放 / Zoom: %d%%  |  实际 / Fit: %d%%", zoom, fit)
        end
        return string.format("缩放: %d%%  |  实际: %d%%", zoom, fit)
    end

    function I18N.KeybindText(name)
        name = tostring(name or "")
        if I18N.lang == "en" then return "Current: " .. name end
        if I18N.lang == "both" then return "当前 / Current: " .. name end
        return "当前: " .. name
    end

    function I18N.DeviceText(device)
        device = tostring(device or "PC")
        if I18N.lang == "en" then return "Device Spoof: " .. device end
        if I18N.lang == "both" then return "设备伪装 / Device: " .. device end
        return "设备伪装: " .. device
    end

    function I18N.QuickButtonText(active)
        if I18N.lang == "en" then
            return "Auto Block\n" .. (active and "ON" or "OFF")
        elseif I18N.lang == "both" then
            return "自动格挡 / Auto Block\n" .. (active and "开 ON" or "关 OFF")
        end
        return "自动格挡\n" .. (active and "开" or "关")
    end

    function I18N.FpsText(fps, ping)
        if I18N.lang == "en" then
            return "FPS: " .. tostring(fps) .. " | PING: " .. tostring(ping) .. " ms"
        elseif I18N.lang == "both" then
            return "帧率/FPS: " .. tostring(fps) .. " | 延迟/Ping: " .. tostring(ping) .. " ms"
        end
        return "帧率: " .. tostring(fps) .. " | 延迟: " .. tostring(ping) .. " ms"
    end

    function I18N.AnimCountText(count)
        count = tonumber(count) or 0
        if I18N.lang == "en" then
            return string.format("Auto-Adapt Guest Skins: %d anim IDs loaded", count)
        elseif I18N.lang == "both" then
            return string.format("自动适配宾客皮肤 / Auto-Adapt: %d anim IDs", count)
        end
        return string.format("自动适配宾客皮肤: 已加载 %d 个动画 ID", count)
    end

    function I18N.LoadSavedLanguage()
        local wanted = nil
        if type(readfile) == "function" then
            local name = "Default"
            local okLast, last = pcall(readfile, "TURNsaken/last.txt")
            if okLast and type(last) == "string" then
                local cleaned = last:gsub("%s+", "")
                if cleaned ~= "" then name = cleaned end
            end
            local okData, data = pcall(readfile, "TURNsaken/" .. name .. ".cfg")
            if okData and type(data) == "string" then
                local raw = data:match("[\r\n]Language=([^\r\n]+)") or data:match("^Language=([^\r\n]+)")
                if raw then
                    raw = raw:gsub("^s:", "")
                    if I18N.Languages[raw] then wanted = raw end
                end
            end
        end
        if wanted then I18N.lang = wanted end
        return I18N.lang
    end

    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- White liquid-glass license gate
    -- ------------------------------------------------------------------
    local function uiCorner(object, radius)
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 12)
        corner.Parent = object
        return corner
    end

    local function uiStroke(object, color, thickness, transparency)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        stroke.Thickness = thickness or 1
        stroke.Transparency = transparency or 0
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        stroke.Parent = object
        return stroke
    end

    local function uiShadow(object, blur, spread, transparency, offsetY)
        pcall(function()
            local shadow = Instance.new("UIShadow")
            shadow.BlurRadius = blur or 24
            shadow.SpreadRadius = spread or 4
            shadow.Transparency = transparency or 0.4
            shadow.Color = Color3.fromRGB(30, 35, 60)
            shadow.Offset = Vector2.new(0, offsetY or 10)
            shadow.Parent = object
        end)
    end

    local function uiTween(object, properties, duration, style, direction, repeatCount, reverses, delayTime)
        pcall(function()
            local info = TweenInfo.new(
                duration or 0.2,
                style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
                direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
                repeatCount or 0,
                reverses or false,
                delayTime or 0
            )
            TweenService:Create(object, info, properties):Play()
        end)
    end

    function TURNsakenShowKeyGate()
        local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
        local LightingService = game:GetService("Lighting")
        local localPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
        local playerGui = localPlayer and localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 20)
        if not playerGui then return false end

        pcall(function()
            local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("TURNsakenKeyUI")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            local oldBlur = LightingService:FindFirstChild("TURNsakenKeyBlur")
            if oldBlur then oldBlur:Destroy() end
        end)

        local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        screenGui.Name = "TURNsakenKeyUI"
        screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        screenGui.DisplayOrder = 9999
        screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        screenGui.Parent = playerGui

        local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
        blur.Name = "TURNsakenKeyBlur"
        blur.Size = 18
        blur.Parent = LightingService

        local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
        overlay.Name = "Overlay"
        overlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 26)
        overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
        overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
        overlay.ZIndex = 0
        overlay.Parent = screenGui
        pcall(function() overlay.Active = true end)

        local overlayGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        overlayGradient.Rotation = 90
        overlayGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(54, 51, 72)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.fromRGB(24, 23, 34)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 14)),
        })
        overlayGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.08),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 0.2),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.32),
        })
        overlayGradient.Parent = overlay

        -- Soft colour clouds behind the glass.
        local function cloud(size, color, position, transparency)
            local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
            frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
            frame.Position = position
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = color
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = transparency
            frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame.ZIndex = 1
            frame.Parent = overlay
            uiCorner(frame, math.floor(size / 2))
            return frame
        end

        local cloudA = cloud(430, Color3.fromRGB(170, 190, 255), UDim2.fromScale(-0.08, -0.12), 0.72)
        local cloudB = cloud(360, Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 225), UDim2.fromScale(0.72, 0.68), 0.78)
        local cloudC = cloud(300, Color3.fromRGB(190, 255, 240), UDim2.fromScale(0.78, -0.08), 0.86)
        uiTween(cloudA, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(-0.02, -0.06)}, 7, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true)
        uiTween(cloudB, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.64, 0.58)}, 8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true)
        uiTween(cloudC, {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.68, -0.02)}, 9, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true)

        local viewport = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
        local designW, designH = 430, 346
        local scale = math.min(1, (viewport.X - 24) / designW, (viewport.Y - 24) / designH)
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Name = "CardHolder"
        holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(designW, designH)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
        holder.ZIndex = 9
        holder.Parent = overlay
        local holderScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        holderScale.Scale = math.clamp(scale, 0.62, 1)
        holderScale.Parent = holder

        local card = Instance.new("Frame")
        card.Name = "GlassCard"
        card.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        card.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 255)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.ClipsDescendants = true
        card.ZIndex = 10
        card.Parent = holder
        uiCorner(card, 27)
        local cardStroke = uiStroke(card, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1.7, 0.14)
        uiShadow(card, 52, 12, 0.4, 20)

        local cardScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        cardScale.Scale = 1
        cardScale.Parent = card

        local cardGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        cardGradient.Rotation = 125
        cardGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.46, Color3.fromRGB(246, 247, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(230, 235, 250)),
        })
        cardGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.02),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.24),
        })
        cardGradient.Parent = card

        local function innerBlob(size, color, position, transparency)
            local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
            frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
            frame.Position = position
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = color
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = transparency
            frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame.ZIndex = 2
            frame.Parent = card
            uiCorner(frame, math.floor(size / 2))
            return frame
        end

        local blobA = innerBlob(210, Color3.fromRGB(208, 220, 255), UDim2.fromOffset(-80, -70), 0.76)
        local blobB = innerBlob(180, Color3.fromRGB(255, 224, 242), UDim2.fromOffset(300, 230), 0.8)
        uiTween(blobA, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-40, -30)}, 6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true)
        uiTween(blobB, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(270, 200)}, 7, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true)

        -- Moving specular highlight (the "liquid" part).
        local shine = Instance.new("Frame")
        shine.Name = "Specular"
        shine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 1.65, 0)
        shine.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-180, -90)
        shine.Rotation = 18
        shine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        shine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
        shine.BorderSizePixel = 0
        shine.ZIndex = 3
        shine.Parent = card
        uiCorner(shine, 48)
        local shineGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        shineGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        })
        shineGradient.Parent = shine
        task.spawn(function()
            while screenGui.Parent do
                shine.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-180, -90)
                uiTween(shine, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(designW + 120, -90)}, 4.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
                task.wait(5.4)
            end
        end)

        local topLine = Instance.new("Frame")
        topLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0, 1)
        topLine.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 1)
        topLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        topLine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        topLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
        topLine.ZIndex = 4
        topLine.Parent = card

        local content = Instance.new("Frame")
        content.Name = "Content"
        content.Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 1, -46)
        content.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 24)
        content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        content.BorderSizePixel = 0
        content.ZIndex = 6
        content.Parent = card

        -- Icon tile
        local iconTile = Instance.new("Frame")
        iconTile.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 48)
        iconTile.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        iconTile.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        iconTile.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
        iconTile.BorderSizePixel = 0
        iconTile.ZIndex = 7
        iconTile.Parent = content
        uiCorner(iconTile, 15)
        uiStroke(iconTile, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1.2, 0.25)
        local iconText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        iconText.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        iconText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        iconText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        iconText.TextSize = 23
        iconText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 36, 54)
        iconText.Text = "T"
        iconText.ZIndex = 8
        iconText.Parent = iconTile

        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 25)
        title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 0)
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        title.TextSize = 19
        title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 29, 42)
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        title.Text = "TURNsaken V3"
        title.ZIndex = 7
        title.Parent = content

        local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 17)
        subtitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 26)
        subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        subtitle.TextSize = 11
        subtitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130)
        subtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        subtitle.Text = I18N.Choose("License Verification", "卡密验证")
        subtitle.ZIndex = 7
        subtitle.Parent = content

        local inputGlow = Instance.new("Frame")
        inputGlow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 10, 0, 54)
        inputGlow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-5, 70)
        inputGlow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 164, 255)
        inputGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        inputGlow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        inputGlow.ZIndex = 6
        inputGlow.Parent = content
        uiCorner(inputGlow, 18)

        local inputWrap = Instance.new("Frame")
        inputWrap.Name = "LicenseInputWrap"
        inputWrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46)
        inputWrap.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 74)
        inputWrap.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        inputWrap.BackgroundTransparency = 0.34
        inputWrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
        inputWrap.ZIndex = 7
        inputWrap.Parent = content
        uiCorner(inputWrap, 14)
        local inputStroke = uiStroke(inputWrap, Color3.fromRGB(126, 126, 148), 1.2, 0.42)

        local inputBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
        inputBox.Name = "LicenseInput"
        inputBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, 0)
        inputBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 0)
        inputBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        inputBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        inputBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        inputBox.TextSize = 14
        inputBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 36, 54)
        inputBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(135, 135, 152)
        inputBox.PlaceholderText = I18N.Choose("Enter license key...", "请输入卡密…")
        inputBox.Text = ""
        inputBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        inputBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        inputBox.ZIndex = 9
        inputBox.Parent = inputWrap

        local status = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        status.Name = "Status"
        status.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
        status.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 130)
        status.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        status.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        status.TextSize = 11
        status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130)
        status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        status.Text = I18N.Choose("Enter your license key to continue", "请输入卡密后继续")
        status.ZIndex = 7
        status.Parent = content

        local action = Instance.new("TextButton")
        action.Name = "Unlock"
        action.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46)
        action.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 158)
        action.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(39, 37, 54)
        action.BorderSizePixel = 0
        action.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        action.TextSize = 14
        action.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        action.Text = I18N.Choose("Unlock", "解锁")
        action.AutoButtonColor = false
        action.ClipsDescendants = true
        action.ZIndex = 8
        action.Parent = content
        uiCorner(action, 14)
        uiStroke(action, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1, 0.2)
        local actionGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        actionGradient.Rotation = 90
        actionGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(62, 58, 82)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(31, 29, 44)),
        })
        actionGradient.Parent = action
        local actionScaleObject = Instance.new("UIScale")
        actionScaleObject.Scale = 1
        actionScaleObject.Parent = action

        local footer = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        footer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
        footer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -24)
        footer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        footer.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        footer.TextSize = 10
        footer.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 127, 145)
        footer.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        footer.Text = "TURNsaken V3  ·  " .. I18N.Choose("Secure License", "安全授权")
        footer.ZIndex = 7
        footer.Parent = content

        local hintBase = I18N.Choose("Enter your license key to continue", "请输入卡密后继续")
        local function setStatus(text, color)
            status.Text = text
            status.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130)
            status.TextTransparency = 0
        end

        local function resetInputLook()
            pcall(function()
                inputStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(126, 126, 148)
                inputStroke.Transparency = 0.42
                inputWrap.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            end)
        end

        local function shakeCard()
            local base = card.Position
            task.spawn(function()
                for _, offset in ipairs({10, -10, 7, -6, 3, -2, 0}) do
                    card.Position = UDim2.new(base.X.Scale, base.X.Offset + offset, base.Y.Scale, base.Y.Offset)
                    task.wait(0.032)
                end
                card.Position = base
            end)
        end

        local function ripple()
            local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
            circle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            circle.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            circle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
            circle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            circle.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
            circle.BorderSizePixel = 0
            circle.ZIndex = 7
            circle.Parent = action
            uiCorner(circle, 80)
            uiTween(circle, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 260), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.45)
            task.delay(0.5, function() pcall(function() circle:Destroy() end) end)
        end

        local function fadeEverything()
            local duration = 0.38
            for _, item in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
                if item:IsA("TextLabel") or item:IsA("TextButton") or item:IsA("TextBox") then
                    uiTween(item, {TextTransparency = 1}, duration)
                end
                if item:IsA("Frame") or item:IsA("TextButton") or item:IsA("TextBox") then
                    uiTween(item, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, duration)
                end
                if item:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    uiTween(item, {Transparency = 1}, duration)
                end
                if item:IsA("UIGradient") then
                    uiTween(item, {Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)}, duration)
                end
            end
            pcall(function() uiTween(blur, {Size = 0}, duration) end)
            task.wait(duration + 0.08)
            pcall(function() if screenGui.Parent then screenGui:Destroy() end end)
            pcall(function() if blur.Parent then blur:Destroy() end end)
        end

        local attempts = 0
        local busy = false
        local unlocked = false
        local expectedKey = "TURNsaken" .. "V3"

        local submit
        submit = function()
            if busy or unlocked then return end
            local cleaned = tostring(inputBox.Text or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
            if cleaned == "" then
                setStatus(I18N.Choose("Please enter a license key", "请输入卡密"), Color3.fromRGB(205, 90, 90))
                pcall(function() inputStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(225, 120, 120) end)
                shakeCard()
                return
            end
            if cleaned == expectedKey or cleaned:lower() == expectedKey:lower() then
                busy = true
                unlocked = true
                setStatus(I18N.Choose("Verified. Loading...", "验证成功，正在加载…"), Color3.fromRGB(32, 148, 102))
                pcall(function()
                    inputBox.TextEditable = false
                    inputStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 178, 128)
                    inputStroke.Transparency = 0.1
                    inputWrap.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 255, 246)
                    action.Text = I18N.Choose("Unlocked", "已解锁")
                end)
                uiTween(cardScale, {Scale = 1.04}, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                task.delay(0.16, function() pcall(fadeEverything) end)
                return
            end

            attempts = attempts + 1
            busy = true
            setStatus(I18N.Choose("Invalid license key. Try again.", "卡密错误，请重试"), Color3.fromRGB(205, 80, 80))
            pcall(function()
                inputStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(228, 110, 110)
                inputStroke.Transparency = 0.1
                inputWrap.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 240)
            end)
            shakeCard()
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.42)
                if unlocked then return end
                resetInputLook()
                if attempts < 3 then
                    busy = false
                    setStatus(hintBase, Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130))
                else
                    local remain = 2
                    while remain > 0 and not unlocked do
                        setStatus(I18N.Choose("Too many attempts. Wait " .. remain .. "s", "尝试次数过多，请等待 " .. remain .. " 秒"), Color3.fromRGB(205, 80, 80))
                        task.wait(1)
                        remain = remain - 1
                    end
                    attempts = 0
                    busy = false
                    if not unlocked then setStatus(hintBase, Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130)) end
                end
            end)
        end

        inputBox.Focused:Connect(function()
            pcall(function()
                uiTween(inputWrap, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.22}, 0.18)
                uiTween(inputGlow, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.86}, 0.22)
                inputStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(74, 72, 98)
                inputStroke.Transparency = 0.2
            end)
            if not busy and not unlocked then setStatus(hintBase, Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130)) end
        end)

        inputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
            pcall(function()
                uiTween(inputWrap, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.34}, 0.18)
                uiTween(inputGlow, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.22)
                if not busy and not unlocked then resetInputLook() end
            end)
            if enterPressed then submit() end
        end)

        inputBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            if busy or unlocked then return end
            pcall(function()
                inputGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.84
                uiTween(inputGlow, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.38)
            end)
            if status.TextColor3 ~= Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130) then
                setStatus(hintBase, Color3.fromRGB(112, 111, 130))
            end
        end)

        action.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            pcall(function() uiTween(actionScaleObject, {Scale = 1.012}, 0.15) end)
        end)
        action.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            pcall(function() uiTween(actionScaleObject, {Scale = 1}, 0.15) end)
        end)
        action.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                pcall(function() uiTween(actionScaleObject, {Scale = 0.972}, 0.08) end)
            end
        end)
        action.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                pcall(function() uiTween(actionScaleObject, {Scale = 1}, 0.14) end)
            end
        end)
        action.Activated:Connect(function()
            ripple()
            submit()
        end)

        -- Subtle card entrance.
        pcall(function()
            cardScale.Scale = 0.94
            card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            cardStroke.Transparency = 1
            uiTween(cardScale, {Scale = 1}, 0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            uiTween(card, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.12}, 0.35)
            uiTween(cardStroke, {Transparency = 0.14}, 0.35)
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.55)
            pcall(function() if inputBox and inputBox.Parent then inputBox:CaptureFocus() end end)
        end)

        while not unlocked do
            if not screenGui.Parent then return false end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        task.wait(0.8)
        pcall(function() if screenGui.Parent then screenGui:Destroy() end end)
        pcall(function() if blur.Parent then blur:Destroy() end end)
        return true
    end

    I18N.LoadSavedLanguage()
    return I18N
end)()

do
    local gateOk, gateResult = pcall(TURNsakenShowKeyGate)
    if not gateOk then
        warn("[TURNsaken] License UI failed to start: " .. tostring(gateResult))
        return
    end
    if not gateResult then
        warn("[TURNsaken] License verification was not completed. Script stopped.")
        return
    end
end
print("[TURNsaken] License verified. Loading features...")

local CustomImageManager = {}
local CustomImageManagerAssets = {
    TransparencyTexture = {
        RobloxId = 139785960036434,
        Path = "Obsidian/assets/TransparencyTexture.png",
        URL = BaseURL .. "assets/TransparencyTexture.png",

        Id = nil,

do
    local function RecursiveCreatePath(Path: string, IsFile: boolean?)
        if not isfolder or not makefolder then
            return
        end

        local Segments = Path:split("/")
        local TraversedPath = ""

        if IsFile then
            table.remove(Segments, #Segments)
        end

        for _, Segment in ipairs(Segments) do
            if not isfolder(TraversedPath .. Segment) then
                makefolder(TraversedPath .. Segment)
            end

            TraversedPath = TraversedPath .. Segment .. "/"
        end

        return TraversedPath
    end

    function CustomImageManager.AddAsset(
        AssetName: string,
        RobloxAssetId: number,
        URL: string,
        ForceRedownload: boolean?
    )
        if CustomImageManagerAssets[AssetName] ~= nil then
            error(string.format("Asset %q already exists", AssetName))
        end

        assert(typeof(RobloxAssetId) == "number", "RobloxAssetId must be a number")

        CustomImageManagerAssets[AssetName] = {
            RobloxId = RobloxAssetId,
            Path = string.format("Obsidian/custom_assets/%s", AssetName),
            URL = URL,

            Id = nil,
        }

        CustomImageManager.DownloadAsset(AssetName, ForceRedownload)
    end

    function CustomImageManager.GetAsset(AssetName: string)
        if not CustomImageManagerAssets[AssetName] then
            return nil
        end

        local AssetData = CustomImageManagerAssets[AssetName]
        if AssetData.Id then
            return AssetData.Id
        end

        local AssetID = string.format("rbxassetid://%s", AssetData.RobloxId)

        if getcustomasset then
            local Success, NewID = pcall(getcustomasset, AssetData.Path)

            if Success and NewID then
                AssetID = NewID
            end
        end

        AssetData.Id = AssetID
        return AssetID
    end

    function CustomImageManager.DownloadAsset(AssetName: string, ForceRedownload: boolean?)
        if not getcustomasset or not writefile or not isfile then
            return false, "missing functions"
        end

        local AssetData = CustomImageManagerAssets[AssetName]

        RecursiveCreatePath(AssetData.Path, true)

        if ForceRedownload ~= true and isfile(AssetData.Path) then
            return true, nil
        end

        local success, errorMessage = pcall(function()
            writefile(AssetData.Path, game:HttpGet(AssetData.URL))
        end)

        return success, errorMessage
    end

    for AssetName, _ in CustomImageManagerAssets do
        CustomImageManager.DownloadAsset(AssetName)
    end
end

--// Scheme Functions \\--
local SchemeReplaceAlias = {
    RedColor = "Red",
    WhiteColor = "White",
    DarkColor = "Dark"
}

local SchemeAlias = {
    Red = "RedColor",
    White = "WhiteColor",
    Dark = "DarkColor"
}

local function GetSchemeValue(Index)
    if not Index then
        return nil
    end

    local ReplaceAliasIndex = SchemeReplaceAlias[Index]
    if ReplaceAliasIndex and Library.Scheme[ReplaceAliasIndex] ~= nil then
        Library.Scheme[Index] = Library.Scheme[ReplaceAliasIndex]
        Library.Scheme[ReplaceAliasIndex] = nil

        return Library.Scheme[Index]
    end

    local AliasIndex = SchemeAlias[Index]
    if AliasIndex and Library.Scheme[AliasIndex] ~= nil then
        warn(string.format("Scheme Value %q is deprecated, please use %q instead.", Index, AliasIndex))
        return Library.Scheme[AliasIndex]
    end

    return Library.Scheme[Index]
end

local function GetTextStrokeColor(): Color3
    local Color = Library.Scheme.FontColor
    return (Color.R * 0.299 + Color.G * 0.587 + Color.B * 0.114) > 0.5 and Library.Scheme.DarkColor or Library.Scheme.WhiteColor
end

--// Basic Functions \\--
local function WaitForEvent(Event, Timeout, Condition)
    local Bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
    local Connection = Event:Once(function(...)
        if not Condition or typeof(Condition) == "function" and Condition(...) then
            Bindable:Fire(true)
        else
            Bindable:Fire(false)
        end
    end)
    task.delay(Timeout, function()
        Connection:Disconnect()
        Bindable:Fire(false)
    end)

    local Result = Bindable.Event:Wait()
    Bindable:Destroy()

    return Result
end

local function IsMouseInput(Input: InputObject, IncludeM2: boolean?)
    return Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or (IncludeM2 == true and Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2)
        or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end
local function IsClickInput(Input: InputObject, IncludeM2: boolean?)
    return IsMouseInput(Input, IncludeM2)
        and Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin
        and Library.IsRobloxFocused
end
local function IsHoverInput(Input: InputObject)
    return (Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
        and Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Change
end
local function IsDragInput(Input: InputObject, IncludeM2: boolean?)
    return IsMouseInput(Input, IncludeM2)
        and (Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin or Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Change)
        and Library.IsRobloxFocused
end
local function IsMouseClickInput(Input: InputObject)
    return Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
        Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 or
        Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3
end
local function IsMovementInput(Input: InputObject)
    return (Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
        and Library.IsRobloxFocused
end

local function GetTableSize(Table: { [any]: any })
    local Size = 0

    for _, _ in Table do
        Size += 1
    end

    return Size
end
local function IsSequentialArray(Table: { [any]: any })
    for Key in Table do
        if typeof(Key) ~= "number" or Key < 1 or Key % 1 ~= 0 then
            return false
        end
    end

    return true
end

local function StopTween(Tween: TweenBase, Destroy: boolean?)
    if not Tween then
        return
    end

    if Tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing then
        Tween:Cancel()
    end

    if Destroy == true then
        pcall(Tween.Destroy, Tween)
    end
end

local function Trim(Text: string)
    return Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
end
local function Round(Value, Rounding)
    assert(Rounding >= 0, "Invalid rounding number.")

    if Rounding == 0 then
        return math.floor(Value)
    end

    return tonumber(string.format("%." .. Rounding .. "f", Value))
end

--// Fuzzy Search \\--
local function LowerText(Text: string): string
    return (Text:lower():gsub("[\195\206\208][\128-\191]", function(Char)
        local Code = utf8.codepoint(Char)
        if (Code >= 0xC0 and Code <= 0xDE and Code ~= 0xD7) or (Code >= 0x391 and Code <= 0x3AB) or (Code >= 0x410 and Code <= 0x42F) then
            return utf8.char(Code + 32)
        elseif Code >= 0x400 and Code <= 0x40F then
            return utf8.char(Code + 80)
        end

        return Char
    end))
end

local function FuzzyScore(Text: string, Search: string): (boolean, number)
    if Search == "" then
        return true, 0
    end
    if Text == "" then
        return false, 0
    end

    --// Fast path: literal substring match (also the best possible score) \\--
    local ExactIdx = Text:find(Search, 1, true)
    if ExactIdx then
        local PrevChar = ExactIdx > 1 and Text:sub(ExactIdx - 1, ExactIdx - 1) or ""
        local AtBoundary = ExactIdx == 1 or PrevChar:match("[%s%p_]") ~= nil

        return true, 1e5 - ExactIdx + (AtBoundary and 500 or 0) + (Search:len() * 5)
    end

    --// Fallback: fuzzy, in-order, non-consecutive character matching \\--
    local TextChars, SearchChars = {}, {}
    for Char in Text:gmatch(utf8.charpattern) do table.insert(TextChars, Char) end
    for Char in Search:gmatch(utf8.charpattern) do table.insert(SearchChars, Char) end

    local TextLen, SearchLen = #TextChars, #SearchChars
    if SearchLen > TextLen then
        return false, 0
    end

    local SearchIdx = 1
    local Score = 0
    local RunLength = 0
    local LastMatchIdx = 0

    for TextIdx = 1, TextLen do
        if SearchIdx > SearchLen then
            break
        end

        if TextChars[TextIdx] == SearchChars[SearchIdx] then
            local PrevChar = TextIdx > 1 and TextChars[TextIdx - 1] or ""
            local AtBoundary = TextIdx == 1 or PrevChar:match("[%s%p_]") ~= nil

            RunLength = (LastMatchIdx == TextIdx - 1) and (RunLength + 1) or 1
            Score += 1 + (AtBoundary and 6 or 0) + math.min(RunLength - 1, 5) * 3

            LastMatchIdx = TextIdx
            SearchIdx += 1
        end
    end

    if SearchIdx <= SearchLen then
        return false, 0 --// Not every Search character was found, in order
    end

    Score -= (LastMatchIdx - SearchLen) * 0.05 --// Slightly favour tighter matches
    return true, Score
end

local function NormalizeSearch(Search: string): string
    return (Search:gsub("%s+", ""))
end

local function TryFuzzyMatch(Text: any, Search: string): (boolean, number)
    if typeof(Text) ~= "string" or Text == "" then
        return false, 0
    end

    return FuzzyScore(LowerText(Text), Search)
end

local function FuzzyMatchScore(Text: any, Search: string): number
    if typeof(Text) ~= "string" or Text == "" then
        return 0
    end

    local Normalized = NormalizeSearch(LowerText(Text))
    local Matched, Score = FuzzyScore(Normalized, Search)
    if not Matched then
        return 0
    end

    if Normalized == Search then
        Score += 1000
    end

    return Score
end

local function MatchesSearch(ElementInfo, Search: string, ForceMatch: boolean?): boolean
    if not ElementInfo then
        return false
    end
    if ForceMatch then
        return true
    end

    if TryFuzzyMatch(ElementInfo.Text, Search) then
        return true
    end
    if TryFuzzyMatch(ElementInfo.Tooltip, Search) then
        return true
    end
    if TryFuzzyMatch(ElementInfo.DisabledTooltip, Search) then
        return true
    end

    --// Optional: search inside Dropdown value lists, so e.g. searching a specific option name reveals the Dropdown that contains it \\--
    if typeof(ElementInfo.Values) == "table" then
        local Checked = 0
        for Key, Value in ElementInfo.Values do
            Checked += 1
            if Checked > 200 then
                break
            end

            if TryFuzzyMatch(Value, Search) or (typeof(Value) ~= "string" and TryFuzzyMatch(tostring(Value), Search)) then
                return true
            end
            if typeof(Key) == "string" and TryFuzzyMatch(Key, Search) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

local function GetPlayers(ExcludeLocalPlayer: boolean?)
    local PlayerList = Players:GetPlayers()

    if ExcludeLocalPlayer then
        local Idx = table.find(PlayerList, LocalPlayer)
        if Idx then
            table.remove(PlayerList, Idx)
        end
    end

    table.sort(PlayerList, function(Player1, Player2)
        return Player1.Name:lower() < Player2.Name:lower()
    end)

    return PlayerList
end
local function GetTeams()
    local TeamList = Teams:GetTeams()

    table.sort(TeamList, function(Team1, Team2)
        return Team1.Name:lower() < Team2.Name:lower()
    end)

    return TeamList
end

function Library:UpdateDependencyBoxes()
    for _, Depbox in Library.DependencyBoxes do
        Depbox:Update(true)
    end

    if Library.Searching then
        Library:UpdateSearch(Library.SearchText)
    end
end

function Library:UpdateAddons(Parent)
    if not Parent or not Parent.Addons then
        return
    end

    for _, Addon in Parent.Addons do
        Addon:Update()
    end
end

local function CheckDepbox(Box, Search, ForceVisible: boolean?)
    local VisibleElements = 0
    local BestScore = 0

    for _, ElementInfo in Box.Elements do
        if ElementInfo.Type == "Divider" then
            ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = false
            continue
        elseif ElementInfo.SubButton then
            --// Check if any of the Buttons Name matches with Search
            local Visible = false

            --// Check if Search matches Element's Name and if Element is Visible
            if MatchesSearch(ElementInfo, Search, ForceVisible) and ElementInfo.Visible then
                Visible = true
                BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.Text, Search))
            else
                ElementInfo.Base.Visible = false
            end
            if MatchesSearch(ElementInfo.SubButton, Search, ForceVisible) and ElementInfo.SubButton.Visible then
                Visible = true
                BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.SubButton.Text, Search))
            else
                ElementInfo.SubButton.Base.Visible = false
            end
            ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = Visible
            if Visible then
                VisibleElements += 1
            end

            continue
        end

        --// Check if Search matches Element's Name and if Element is Visible
        if ElementInfo.Text and MatchesSearch(ElementInfo, Search, ForceVisible) and ElementInfo.Visible then
            ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = true
            VisibleElements += 1
            BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.Text, Search))
        else
            ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = false
        end
    end

    for _, Depbox in Box.DependencyBoxes do
        if not Depbox.Visible then
            continue
        end

        local DepVisible, DepScore = CheckDepbox(Depbox, Search, ForceVisible)
        VisibleElements += DepVisible
        if DepScore > BestScore then
            BestScore = DepScore
        end
    end

    Box.Holder.Visible = VisibleElements > 0
    return VisibleElements, BestScore
end
local function RestoreDepbox(Box)
    for _, ElementInfo in Box.Elements do
        ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = ElementInfo.Visible ~= false

        if ElementInfo.SubButton then
            ElementInfo.Base.Visible = ElementInfo.Visible
            ElementInfo.SubButton.Base.Visible = ElementInfo.SubButton.Visible
        end
    end

    for _, Depbox in Box.DependencyBoxes do
        RestoreDepbox(Depbox)
    end

    Box.Holder.Visible = Box.Visible
    if Box.Visible then
        Box:Resize()
    end
end

--// Pop Out
function SyncPopOutVisibility(Box: any)
    if not Box.PopOutFloat then
        return
    end

    Box.PopOutFloat.Visible = Box.BoxHolder.Visible ~= false and Box.Visible ~= false
end

local function RegisterPopOutClone(Source: Instance, Clone: Instance)
    local SourceProps = Library.Registry[Source]
    if SourceProps then
        local CloneProps = {}
        for Property, Index in SourceProps do
            if Property ~= "FontFace" then
                CloneProps[Property] = Index
            end
        end
        if next(CloneProps) then
            Library.Registry[Clone] = CloneProps
        end
    end

    local SourceChildren = Source:GetChildren()
    local CloneChildren = Clone:GetChildren()
    for Index = 1, math.min(#SourceChildren, #CloneChildren) do
        RegisterPopOutClone(SourceChildren[Index], CloneChildren[Index])
    end
end

local function DimPopOutClone(Root: GuiObject)
    for _, Descendant in Root:QueryDescendants("TextLabel, TextButton, TextBox") do
        Descendant.TextTransparency = math.max(Descendant.TextTransparency, 0.45)
    end

    for _, Descendant in Root:QueryDescendants("ImageLabel, ImageButton") do
        Descendant.ImageTransparency = math.max(Descendant.ImageTransparency, 0.45)
    end

    for _, Descendant in Root:QueryDescendants("GuiButton") do
        Descendant.Active = false
        Descendant.AutoButtonColor = false
    end
end

local function IsScreenPointOutsideMain(Point: Vector2): boolean
    local MainFrame = Library.Window and Library.Window.MainFrame
    if not MainFrame or not Library.Toggled or not MainFrame.Visible then
        return true
    end

    return not Library:MouseIsOverFrame(MainFrame, Point)
end

local function GetTopFloatAt(Point: Vector2): GuiObject?
    local Best: GuiObject? = nil
    local BestOrder = -math.huge
    local Floats = Library.Floats

    for _, Surface in Library.DraggableElements do
        if not Surface or not Surface.Parent or not Surface.Visible then
            continue
        end
        if Floats and Surface.Parent ~= Floats then
            continue
        end
        if not Library:MouseIsOverFrame(Surface, Point) then
            continue
        end

        local SiblingIndex = tonumber(select(2, pcall(function() return Surface:GetSiblingIndex() end))) or 0
        local Order = Surface.ZIndex * 100000 + SiblingIndex
        if Order >= BestOrder then
            BestOrder = Order
            Best = Surface
        end
    end

    return Best
end

local function GetPopOutBodyMaxHeight(Box: any, Reserved: number): number
    local Float = Box.PopOutFloat
    local ScreenGui = Library.ScreenGui
    if not Float or not ScreenGui then
        return math.huge
    end

    local Gap = 12 * Library.DPIScale
    local MaxBottom = ScreenGui.AbsolutePosition.Y + ScreenGui.AbsoluteSize.Y - Gap
    local Available = math.min(MaxBottom - Float.AbsolutePosition.Y, ScreenGui.AbsoluteSize.Y * 0.9)
    local ScreenMax = math.max(0, Available / Library.DPIScale - Reserved)

    local CustomMax = Box.PopOutMaxHeight
    if typeof(CustomMax) == "number" then
        return math.min(ScreenMax, math.max(0, CustomMax))
    end

    return ScreenMax
end

--// Search
local function ApplySearchToTab(Tab, Search)
    if not Tab then
        return false, 0
    end

    local HasVisible = false
    local BestScore = 0

    --// If the Tab itself matches Search (by name/description), don't filter out its contents -- pull everything in the Tab along with it \\--
    local TabMatches = TryFuzzyMatch(Tab.Name, Search) or TryFuzzyMatch(Tab.Description, Search)
    BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(Tab.Name, Search), FuzzyMatchScore(Tab.Description, Search))

    for _, Groupbox in Tab.Groupboxes do
        if Groupbox.Visible == false then
            continue
        end

        --// Optional: matching the Groupbox's own name/description reveals every element inside it, without needing each one to match too
        local GroupboxMatches = TabMatches or (TryFuzzyMatch(Groupbox.Name, Search) or TryFuzzyMatch(Groupbox.Description, Search))
        BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(Groupbox.Name, Search), FuzzyMatchScore(Groupbox.Description, Search))

        local VisibleElements = 0
        for _, ElementInfo in Groupbox.Elements do
            if ElementInfo.Type == "Divider" then
                ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = false
                continue
            elseif ElementInfo.SubButton then
                --// Check if any of the Buttons Name matches with Search
                local Visible = false
                if MatchesSearch(ElementInfo, Search, GroupboxMatches) and ElementInfo.Visible then
                    Visible = true
                    BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.Text, Search))
                else
                    ElementInfo.Base.Visible = false
                end

                if MatchesSearch(ElementInfo.SubButton, Search, GroupboxMatches) and ElementInfo.SubButton.Visible then
                    Visible = true
                    BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.SubButton.Text, Search))
                else
                    ElementInfo.SubButton.Base.Visible = false
                end

                ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = Visible
                if Visible then
                    VisibleElements += 1
                end

                continue
            end

            --// Check if Search matches Element's Name and if Element is Visible
            if ElementInfo.Text and MatchesSearch(ElementInfo, Search, GroupboxMatches) and ElementInfo.Visible then
                ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = true
                VisibleElements += 1
                BestScore = math.max(BestScore, FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.Text, Search))
            else
                ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = false
            end
        end

        for _, Depbox in Groupbox.DependencyBoxes do
            if not Depbox.Visible then
                continue
            end

            local DepVisible, DepScore = CheckDepbox(Depbox, Search, GroupboxMatches)
            VisibleElements += DepVisible
            if DepScore > BestScore then
                BestScore = DepScore
            end
        end

        --// Update Groupbox Size and Visibility if found any element
        if VisibleElements > 0 then
            Groupbox:Resize()
            HasVisible = true
        end
        Groupbox.BoxHolder.Visible = VisibleElements > 0
        SyncPopOutVisibility(Groupbox)
    end

    for _, Tabbox in Tab.Tabboxes do
        local VisibleTabs = 0
        local VisibleElements = {}
        local SubTabScores = {}

        for _, SubTab in Tabbox.Tabs do
            VisibleElements[SubTab] = 0

            --// Optional: matching a Tabbox sub-tab's own name reveals every element inside it, without needing each one to match too
            local SubTabMatches = TabMatches or TryFuzzyMatch(SubTab.Name, Search)
            local SubScore = FuzzyMatchScore(SubTab.Name, Search)
            BestScore = math.max(BestScore, SubScore)

            for _, ElementInfo in SubTab.Elements do
                if ElementInfo.Type == "Divider" then
                    ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = false
                    continue
                elseif ElementInfo.SubButton then
                    --// Check if any of the Buttons Name matches with Search
                    local Visible = false
                    if MatchesSearch(ElementInfo, Search, SubTabMatches) and ElementInfo.Visible then
                        Visible = true
                        local ElementScore = FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.Text, Search)
                        SubScore = math.max(SubScore, ElementScore)
                        BestScore = math.max(BestScore, ElementScore)
                    else
                        ElementInfo.Base.Visible = false
                    end

                    if MatchesSearch(ElementInfo.SubButton, Search, SubTabMatches) and ElementInfo.SubButton.Visible then
                        Visible = true
                        local ElementScore = FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.SubButton.Text, Search)
                        SubScore = math.max(SubScore, ElementScore)
                        BestScore = math.max(BestScore, ElementScore)
                    else
                        ElementInfo.SubButton.Base.Visible = false
                    end

                    ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = Visible
                    if Visible then
                        VisibleElements[SubTab] += 1
                    end

                    continue
                end

                --// Check if Search matches Element's Name and if Element is Visible
                if ElementInfo.Text and MatchesSearch(ElementInfo, Search, SubTabMatches) and ElementInfo.Visible then
                    ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = true
                    VisibleElements[SubTab] += 1
                    local ElementScore = FuzzyMatchScore(ElementInfo.Text, Search)
                    SubScore = math.max(SubScore, ElementScore)
                    BestScore = math.max(BestScore, ElementScore)
                else
                    ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = false
                end
            end

            for _, Depbox in SubTab.DependencyBoxes do
                if not Depbox.Visible then
                    continue
                end

                local DepVisible, DepScore = CheckDepbox(Depbox, Search, SubTabMatches)
                VisibleElements[SubTab] += DepVisible
                SubScore = math.max(SubScore, DepScore)
                BestScore = math.max(BestScore, DepScore)
            end

            SubTabScores[SubTab] = SubScore
        end

        local BestSubTab = nil
        local BestSubScore = -1

        for SubTab, Visible in VisibleElements do
            SubTab.ButtonHolder.Visible = Visible > 0
            if Visible > 0 then
                VisibleTabs += 1
                HasVisible = true

                local SubScore = SubTabScores[SubTab] or 0
                if SubScore > BestSubScore then
                    BestSubScore = SubScore
                    BestSubTab = SubTab
                end
            end
        end

        local ActiveSubTab = Tabbox.ActiveTab
        local ActiveSubVisible = ActiveSubTab and (VisibleElements[ActiveSubTab] or 0) > 0
        local ActiveSubScore = ActiveSubTab and (SubTabScores[ActiveSubTab] or -1) or -1

        if ActiveSubVisible and ActiveSubScore >= BestSubScore then
            ActiveSubTab:Resize()
        elseif BestSubTab then
            BestSubTab:Show()
        end

        --// Update Tabbox Visibility if any visible
        Tabbox.BoxHolder.Visible = VisibleTabs > 0
        SyncPopOutVisibility(Tabbox)
    end

    return HasVisible, BestScore
end

local function ResetTab(Tab)
    if not Tab then
        return
    end

    for _, Groupbox in Tab.Groupboxes do
        for _, ElementInfo in Groupbox.Elements do
            ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = ElementInfo.Visible ~= false

            if ElementInfo.SubButton then
                ElementInfo.Base.Visible = ElementInfo.Visible
                ElementInfo.SubButton.Base.Visible = ElementInfo.SubButton.Visible
            end
        end

        for _, Depbox in Groupbox.DependencyBoxes do
            RestoreDepbox(Depbox)
        end

        Groupbox:Resize()
        Groupbox.BoxHolder.Visible = Groupbox.Visible ~= false
        SyncPopOutVisibility(Groupbox)
    end

    for _, Tabbox in Tab.Tabboxes do
        for _, SubTab in Tabbox.Tabs do
            for _, ElementInfo in SubTab.Elements do
                ElementInfo.Holder.Visible = ElementInfo.Visible ~= false

                if ElementInfo.SubButton then
                    ElementInfo.Base.Visible = ElementInfo.Visible
                    ElementInfo.SubButton.Base.Visible = ElementInfo.SubButton.Visible
                end
            end

            for _, Depbox in SubTab.DependencyBoxes do
                RestoreDepbox(Depbox)
            end

            SubTab.ButtonHolder.Visible = true
        end

        if Tabbox.ActiveTab then
            Tabbox.ActiveTab:Resize()
        end
        Tabbox.BoxHolder.Visible = true
        SyncPopOutVisibility(Tabbox)
    end
end

function Library:UpdateSearch(SearchText)
    Library.SearchText = SearchText

    local TabsToSearch = {}
    for _, Tab in Library.Tabs do
        if typeof(Tab) == "table" and not Tab.IsKeyTab and not Tab.IsCustomTab then
            table.insert(TabsToSearch, Tab)
        end
    end

    for _, Tab in TabsToSearch do
        ResetTab(Tab)
    end

    local Search = NormalizeSearch(LowerText(SearchText))
    if Trim(Search) == "" then
        Library.Searching = false
        Library.LastSearchTab = nil
        return
    end
    if not Library.GlobalSearch and Library.ActiveTab and Library.ActiveTab.IsKeyTab then
        Library.Searching = false
        Library.LastSearchTab = nil
        return
    end

    Library.Searching = true

    local BestTab = nil
    local BestScore = -1
    local ActiveScore = -1
    local ActiveHasVisible = false

    for _, Tab in TabsToSearch do
        local HasVisible, Score = ApplySearchToTab(Tab, Search)
        if not HasVisible then
            continue
        end

        if Tab == Library.ActiveTab then
            ActiveHasVisible = true
            ActiveScore = Score
        end
        if Score > BestScore then
            BestScore = Score
            BestTab = Tab
        end
    end

    if not Library.GlobalSearch then
        for _, Tab in TabsToSearch do
            if Tab ~= BestTab then
                ResetTab(Tab)
            end
        end
    end

    local StayOnActive = ActiveHasVisible and ActiveScore >= BestScore
    if StayOnActive and Library.ActiveTab then
        Library.ActiveTab:RefreshSides()
    elseif BestTab then
        local SearchMarker = SearchText
        task.defer(function()
            if Library.SearchText ~= SearchMarker then
                return
            end

            if Library.ActiveTab ~= BestTab then
                BestTab:Show()
            elseif Library.ActiveTab then
                Library.ActiveTab:RefreshSides()
            end
        end)
    end

    Library.LastSearchTab = nil
end

function Library:AddToRegistry(Instance, Properties)
    Library.Registry[Instance] = Properties
end

function Library:RemoveFromRegistry(Instance)
    Library.Registry[Instance] = nil
end

function Library:UpdateColorsUsingRegistry()
    for Instance, Properties in Library.Registry do
        for Property, Index in Properties do
            local SchemeValue = GetSchemeValue(Index)

            if SchemeValue or typeof(Index) == "function" then
                Instance[Property] = SchemeValue or Index()
            end
        end
    end
end

function Library:SetDPIScale(DPIScale: number)
    Library.DPIScale = DPIScale / 100
    Library.MinSize = Library.OriginalMinSize * Library.DPIScale

    for _, UIScale in Library.Scales do
        UIScale.Scale = Library.DPIScale - (tonumber(Library.ScalesOffset[UIScale]) or 0)
    end

    for _, Option in Options do
        if Option.Type == "Dropdown" then
            Option:RecalculateListSize()
            Option:RefreshPool()
        end
    end

    for _, Notification in Library.Notifications do
        Notification:Resize()
    end
end

function Library:GiveSignal(Connection: RBXScriptConnection | RBXScriptSignal)
    local ConnectionType = typeof(Connection)
    if Connection and (ConnectionType == "RBXScriptConnection" or ConnectionType == "RBXScriptSignal") then
        table.insert(Library.Signals, Connection)
    end

    return Connection
end

function IsValidCustomIcon(Icon: string)
    return typeof(Icon) == "string" and (Icon:match("^rbxasset://textures/") or Icon:match("roblox%.com/asset/%?id=") or Icon:match("rbxthumb://type="))
end

local function IsCustomAssetIcon(Icon: string, IncludeAssetId: boolean)
    return typeof(Icon) == "string" and (Icon:match("^content://") or (Icon:match("^rbxasset://%x+/") or Icon:match("^rbxasset://[^/]+/")) or (IncludeAssetId == true and Icon:match("^rbxassetid://")))
end

type Icon = {
    Url: string,
    Id: number,
    IconName: string,
    ImageRectOffset: Vector2,
    ImageRectSize: Vector2,
    FontFace: Font?,
    Text: string?,
    Custom: boolean?,
}

type IconModule = {
    Icons: { string },
    GetAsset: (Name: string) -> Icon?,
}

local FetchIcons = false
local Icons: IconModule | nil = nil

function Library:GetIcon(IconName: string): Icon?
    if not FetchIcons or not Icons then
        return nil
    end

    local Success, Icon = pcall(Icons.GetAsset, IconName)
    return if Success then Icon else nil
end

function Library:GetCustomIcon(IconName: string): any
    if not IconName then
        return nil
    end

    if tonumber(IconName) then
        IconName = string.format("rbxassetid://%s", tostring(IconName))
    end

    if IsCustomAssetIcon(IconName, true) then
        return {
            Url = IconName,
            ImageRectOffset = Vector2.zero,
            ImageRectSize = Vector2.zero,
        }
    elseif IsValidCustomIcon(IconName) then
        return {
            Url = IconName,
            ImageRectOffset = Vector2.zero,
            ImageRectSize = Vector2.zero,
            Custom = true,
        }
    end

    local LucideIcon = Library:GetIcon(IconName)
    if LucideIcon then
        return LucideIcon
    end

    return nil
end

function Library:Validate(Table: { [string]: any }, Template: { [string]: any }): { [string]: any }
    if typeof(Table) ~= "table" then
        return Template
    end

    for k, v in Template do
        if typeof(k) == "number" then
            continue
        end

        if typeof(v) == "table" then
            Table[k] = Library:Validate(Table[k], v)
        elseif Table[k] == nil then
            Table[k] = v
        end
    end

    return Table
end

--// Creator Functions \\--
local function FillInstance(Table: { [string]: any }, Instance: GuiObject)
    local ThemeProperties = Library.Registry[Instance] or {}

    for key, value in Table do
        if key ~= "Text" then
            local SchemeValue = GetSchemeValue(value)

            if SchemeValue or typeof(value) == "function" then
                ThemeProperties[key] = value
                value = SchemeValue or value()
            else
                ThemeProperties[key] = nil
            end
        end

        Instance[key] = value
    end

    if GetTableSize(ThemeProperties) > 0 then
        Library.Registry[Instance] = ThemeProperties
    end
end

local function New(ClassName: string, Properties: { [string]: any }): any
    local Instance = Instance.new(ClassName)

    if Templates[ClassName] then
        FillInstance(Templates[ClassName], Instance)
    end
    FillInstance(Properties, Instance)

    if Properties["Parent"] and not Properties["ZIndex"] then
        pcall(function()
            Instance.ZIndex = Properties.Parent.ZIndex
        end)
    end

    return Instance
end

--// Icon Instances \\--
local IconTextProperties = { ImageColor3 = "TextColor3", ImageTransparency = "TextTransparency" }
local IconSharedProperties = { "Name", "AnchorPoint", "Position", "Size", "SizeConstraint", "LayoutOrder", "ZIndex", "Visible", "Rotation", "ImageColor3", "ImageTransparency" }

local function IconProperties(ClassName: string, Properties: { [string]: any }): { [string]: any }
    if ClassName ~= "TextLabel" and ClassName ~= "TextButton" then
        return Properties
    end

    local Mapped: { [string]: any } = {}
    for Property, Value in Properties do
        Mapped[IconTextProperties[Property] or Property] = Value
    end

    return Mapped
end

local function IsFontIcon(Icon: Icon?, Size: UDim2?, Parent: Instance?): boolean
    if Icon == nil or Icon.FontFace == nil or Size == nil then
        return false
    end

    local Height = Size.Y.Offset
    if Size.Y.Scale ~= 0 then
        if not (Parent and Parent:IsA("GuiObject")) then
            return false
        end

        Height += Parent.AbsoluteSize.Y / Library.DPIScale * Size.Y.Scale
    end

    return Height > Library.MaxSpriteIconSize
end

local function ImageContent(Icon: Icon?): { [string]: any }
    return {
        Image = Icon and Icon.Url or "",
        ImageRectOffset = Icon and Icon.ImageRectOffset or Vector2.zero,
        ImageRectSize = Icon and Icon.ImageRectSize or Vector2.zero,
    }
end

local function SetIconProperties(Target: GuiObject, Properties: { [string]: any })
    FillInstance(IconProperties(Target.ClassName, Properties), Target)
end

local function TweenIcon(Target: GuiObject, Properties: { [string]: any }): Tween
    local Tween = TweenService:Create(Target, Library.TweenInfo, IconProperties(Target.ClassName, Properties))
    Tween:Play()

    return Tween
end

function Library:CreateIcon(Icon: Icon?, Properties: { [string]: any }?, IsButton: boolean?): GuiObject
    local IsFont = IsFontIcon(Icon, Properties and Properties.Size, Properties and Properties.Parent)
    local ClassName = (if IsFont then "Text" else "Image") .. (if IsButton then "Button" else "Label")

    local Merged: { [string]: any } = {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
    }

    if not IsFont then
        Merged.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    end

    if Properties then
        for Property, Value in Properties do
            Merged[Property] = Value
        end
    end

    return Library:ApplyIcon(New(ClassName, IconProperties(ClassName, Merged)), Icon)
end

function Library:ApplyIcon(Target: GuiObject, Icon: Icon?): GuiObject
    local IsFont = IsFontIcon(Icon, Target.Size, Target.Parent)
    local IsText = Target:IsA("TextLabel") or Target:IsA("TextButton")

    if IsFont ~= IsText then
        local Theme = Library.Registry[Target] or {}
        local Properties: { [string]: any } = { Parent = Target.Parent }

        for _, Property in IconSharedProperties do
            local Source = if IsText then IconTextProperties[Property] or Property else Property
            Properties[Property] = if Theme[Source] ~= nil then Theme[Source] else (Target :: any)[Source]
        end

        local Replacement = Library:CreateIcon(Icon, Properties, Target:IsA("GuiButton"))
        Library:RemoveFromRegistry(Target)
        Target:Destroy()

        return Replacement
    end

    if IsFont and Icon then
        FillInstance({
            FontFace = Icon.FontFace,
            RichText = false,
            Text = Icon.Text,
            TextScaled = true,
        }, Target)

        if not Target:FindFirstChild("IconPadding") then
            New("UIPadding", {
                Name = "IconPadding",
                PaddingLeft = UDim.new(-0.5, 0),
                PaddingRight = UDim.new(-0.5, 0),
                Parent = Target,
            })
        end
    else
        FillInstance(ImageContent(Icon), Target)
    end

    return Target
end

--// DEPRECATED
function Library:ApplyLucideIcon(ImageGui: ImageLabel | ImageButton, Icon: Icon, Rotation: number?)
    warn("Obsidian:ApplyLucideIcon is deprecated, please use Obsidian:CreateIcon or Obsidian:ApplyIcon instead.")

    ImageGui.Rotation = Rotation or ImageGui.Rotation
    FillInstance(ImageContent(Icon), ImageGui)
end

--// Main Instances \\-
local function SafeParentUI(Instance: Instance, Parent: Instance | () -> Instance)
    local success, _error = pcall(function()
        if not Parent then
            Parent = CoreGui
        end

        local DestinationParent
        if typeof(Parent) == "function" then
            DestinationParent = Parent()
        else
            DestinationParent = Parent
        end

        Instance.Parent = DestinationParent
    end)

    if not (success and Instance.Parent) then
        Instance.Parent = Library.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", math.huge)
    end
end

local function ParentUI(UI: Instance, SkipHiddenUI: boolean?)
    if SkipHiddenUI then
        SafeParentUI(UI, CoreGui)
        return
    end

    pcall(protectgui, UI)
    SafeParentUI(UI, gethui)
end

local function SetAlwaysOnTop(Gui: ScreenGui, Enabled: boolean)
    if not Gui then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            sethiddenproperty(Gui, "OnTopOfCoreBlur", Enabled)
        elseif setscriptable then
            setscriptable(Gui, "OnTopOfCoreBlur", true)
            Gui.OnTopOfCoreBlur = Enabled
            setscriptable(Gui, "OnTopOfCoreBlur", false)
        end
    end)
end

    end)
end)

    return (loadstring(
        game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/notpoiu/Molto/refs/heads/main/source.lua")
    ) :: () -> IconModule)()
end)
do
    if OnlineFetchIcons and OnlineIcons then
        OnlineIcons.SetProvider("lucide")
        Library:SetIconModule(OnlineIcons)
    else
        OnlineFetchIcons, OnlineIcons = pcall(function()
            return (loadstring(
                game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/notpoiu/lucide-roblox-direct/refs/heads/main/source.lua")
            ) :: () -> IconModule)()
        end)
        
        if OnlineFetchIcons and OnlineIcons then
            Library:SetIconModule(OnlineIcons)
        end
    end
end

--// Lib Functions \\--
Library.Cursor = {}

function Library.Cursor:ResetCross()
    for _, Inner in InnerCross do
        Library.Registry[Inner].BackgroundColor3 = "WhiteColor"
        Inner.BackgroundColor3 = Library.Scheme.WhiteColor
    end
end

function Library.Cursor:ResetIcon()
    CursorCross.Visible = true
    CursorCustomImage.Visible = false
    CursorCustomImage.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
    SetIconProperties(CursorCustomImage, { ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) })
end

function Library.Cursor:ResetCursor()
    Library.Cursor:ResetCross()
    Library.Cursor:ResetIcon()
end

function Library.Cursor:ChangeCrossColor(Color: Color3)
    assert(typeof(Color) == "Color3", "Color3 expected.")
    for _, Inner in InnerCross do
        Inner.BackgroundColor3 = Color
        Library.Registry[Inner].BackgroundColor3 = nil
    end
end

function Library.Cursor:ChangeIcon(ImageId: string)
    if not ImageId or ImageId == "" then
        Library.Cursor:ResetIcon()
        return
    end

    local Icon = Library:GetCustomIcon(ImageId)
    assert(Icon, "Image must be a valid Roblox asset or a valid URL or a valid lucide icon.")

    CursorCross.Visible = false
    CursorCustomImage = Library:ApplyIcon(CursorCustomImage, Icon)
    CursorCustomImage.Visible = true
end

function Library.Cursor:ChangeIconColor(Color: Color3)
    assert(typeof(Color) == "Color3", "Color3 expected.")
    SetIconProperties(CursorCustomImage, { ImageColor3 = Color })
end

function Library.Cursor:ChangeIconSize(Size: UDim2)
    assert(typeof(Size) == "UDim2", "UDim2 expected.")
    CursorCustomImage.Size = Size
end

--// Colors \\--
function Library:GetBetterColor(Color: Color3, Add: number): Color3
    Add = Add * (Library.IsLightTheme and -4 or 2)
    return Color3.fromRGB(
        math.clamp(Color.R * 255 + Add, 0, 255),
        math.clamp(Color.G * 255 + Add, 0, 255),
        math.clamp(Color.B * 255 + Add, 0, 255)
    )
end

function Library:GetLighterColor(Color: Color3): Color3
    local H, S, V = Color:ToHSV()
    return Color3.fromHSV(H, math.max(0, S - 0.1), math.min(1, V + 0.1))
end

function Library:GetDarkerColor(Color: Color3): Color3
    local H, S, V = Color:ToHSV()
    return Color3.fromHSV(H, S, V / 2)
end

function Library:GetKeyString(KeyCode: Enum.KeyCode)
    if KeyCode.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode and KeyCode.Value > 33 and KeyCode.Value < 127 then
        return string.char(KeyCode.Value)
    end

    return KeyCode.Name
end

function Library:GetTextBounds(Text: string, Font: Font, Size: number, Width: number?): (number, number)
    local Scale = Library.DPIScale
    local Params = Instance.new("GetTextBoundsParams")
    Params.Text = Text
    Params.RichText = true
    Params.Font = Font
    Params.Size = Size * Scale
    if Width then
        Params.Width = Width * Scale
    else
        Params.Width = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X - 32
    end

    local Bounds = TextService:GetTextBoundsAsync(Params)
    return math.ceil(Bounds.X / Scale), math.ceil(Bounds.Y / Scale)
end

function Library:MouseIsOverFrame(Frame: GuiObject, Mouse: Vector2): boolean
    local AbsPos, AbsSize = Frame.AbsolutePosition, Frame.AbsoluteSize
    return Mouse.X >= AbsPos.X
        and Mouse.X <= AbsPos.X + AbsSize.X
        and Mouse.Y >= AbsPos.Y
        and Mouse.Y <= AbsPos.Y + AbsSize.Y
end

function Library:IsInsideFrame(ParentFrame: GuiObject, Frame: GuiObject)
    local GuiPos = Frame.AbsolutePosition
    local GuiSize = Frame.AbsoluteSize

    local FramePos = ParentFrame.AbsolutePosition
    local FrameSize = ParentFrame.AbsoluteSize

    return GuiPos.X >= FramePos.X
        and GuiPos.X + GuiSize.X <= FramePos.X + FrameSize.X
        and GuiPos.Y >= FramePos.Y
        and GuiPos.Y + GuiSize.Y <= FramePos.Y + FrameSize.Y
end

function Library:SafeCallback(Func: (...any) -> ...any, ...: any)
