do
    local added = 0
    local scannedSkins = 0
    local function scanConfig(cfgModule)
        if not cfgModule then return end
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, cfgModule)
        if not ok or not mod then return end
        local anims = mod.Animations
        if not anims then return end
        for _, key in ipairs({"Punch", "ParryPunch"}) do
            local asset = anims[key]
            if asset then
                local id = tostring(asset):match("%d+")
                if id and id ~= "" then
                    punchAnimSet[id] = true
                    PUNCH_ANIM_IDS[id] = true
                    if not table.find(punchAnimIds, id) then
                        table.insert(punchAnimIds, id)
                        added = added + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
        if not assets then return end
        local survs = assets:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
        local guestBase = survs and survs:FindFirstChild("Guest1337")
        if guestBase then scanConfig(guestBase:FindFirstChild("Config")) end
        local skins = assets:FindFirstChild("Skins")
        local skinSurvs = skins and skins:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
        local guestSkins = skinSurvs and skinSurvs:FindFirstChild("Guest1337")
        if guestSkins then
            for _, skin in ipairs(guestSkins:GetChildren()) do
                if skin:IsA("Folder") then
                    scannedSkins = scannedSkins + 1
                    scanConfig(skin:FindFirstChild("Config"))
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    print(string.format("[TURNsaken] Guest skin punch auto-adapt: scanned %d skins, added %d new anim IDs", scannedSkins, added))
end

local autoPunchEventEnabled = false
local function isLocalSurvivor()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return false end
    return char.Parent == SurvivorsFolder
end

local function fireRemoteBlock()
    if testRemote then testRemote:FireServer("UseActorAbility", { buffer.fromstring("\003\005\000\000\000Block") }) end
end

local function fireRemotePunch()
    if testRemote then
        pcall(function() testRemote:FireServer("UseActorAbility", { buffer.fromstring("\003\005\000\000\000Punch") }) end)
    end
end

function TURNsakenCreateSpatialOverlapBox(creatorChar, size, onHitDetected)
    local creatorRoot = creatorChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or creatorChar:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
    if not creatorRoot then return end
    local visualPart
    if TURNsakenShowHitboxVisual then
        visualPart = Instance.new("Part")
        visualPart.Name = "TURNsakenAutoBlockHitbox_Frame"
        visualPart.Size = size
        visualPart.Transparency = TURNsakenHitboxTransparency
        visualPart.CanCollide = false; visualPart.CanTouch = false; visualPart.CanQuery = false; visualPart.CastShadow = false; visualPart.Anchored = true
        visualPart.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0); visualPart.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField; visualPart.Parent = workspace
        if TURNsakenShowOutline then
            local o = Instance.new("SelectionBox", visualPart)
            o.Adornee = visualPart; o.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0); o.LineThickness = TURNsakenOutlineThickness; o.Transparency = 0.1
        end
    end
    local op = OverlapParams.new()
    op.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    op.FilterDescendantsInstances = { creatorChar }
    local vc
    if visualPart then
        vc = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not creatorChar.Parent or not creatorRoot.Parent or not visualPart.Parent then if vc then vc:Disconnect() end; return end
            visualPart.CFrame = creatorRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, TURNsakenHitboxOffset - TURNsakenHitboxFrontBackOffset)
        end)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local hasHit = false; local st = tick()
        while creatorChar.Parent and creatorRoot and creatorRoot.Parent and (tick() - st) < TURNsakenHitboxDuration do
            pcall(function()
                local cc = creatorRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, TURNsakenHitboxOffset - TURNsakenHitboxFrontBackOffset)
                local parts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(cc, size, op)
                local hit = false; local anyHit = false
                for _, v in ipairs(parts) do
                    local h = v.Parent and v.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if h and h.Health > 0 then
                        local p = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(h.Parent)
                        if p then anyHit = true; if p == lp then hit = true end end
                    end
                end
                if visualPart and visualPart.Parent then
                    local tc = anyHit and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                    visualPart.Color = tc
                    local o = visualPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("SelectionBox")
                    if o then o.Color3 = tc end
                end
                if hit and not hasHit then hasHit = true; if onHitDetected then onHitDetected() end end
            end)
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        if vc then vc:Disconnect() end
        if visualPart and visualPart.Parent then visualPart:Destroy() end
    end)
end

local function TURNsakenArmKillerHitbox(killer)
    local bs = TURNsakenBaseHitboxSize * TURNsakenHitboxScale
    local fs = Vector3.new(bs.X * TURNsakenHitboxWidthScale, bs.Y, bs.Z)
    task.spawn(function()
        if TURNsakenHitboxDelay > 0 then
            task.wait(TURNsakenHitboxDelay)
        end
        if killer and killer.Parent then
            TURNsakenCreateSpatialOverlapBox(killer, fs, function()
                fireRemoteBlock()
                if autoPunchEventEnabled then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.18)
                        fireRemotePunch()
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function TURNsakenCheckAbilityAttributes()
    local kf = KillersFolder
    if not kf or #kf:GetChildren() == 0 then
        local pf = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
        local realKf = pf and pf:FindFirstChild("Killers")
        if realKf then kf = realKf end
    end
    local now = tick()
    for _, killer in ipairs(kf:GetChildren()) do
        if killer:IsA("Model") then
            local lastUsed = killer:GetAttribute("AbilityLastUsed") or 0
            local used = killer:GetAttribute("AbilitiesUsed") or 0
            local state = TURNsakenKillerAbilityState[killer]
            if not state then
                TURNsakenKillerAbilityState[killer] = {last = lastUsed, used = used, lastTrigger = 0}
            else
                local reset = (lastUsed < state.last) or (used < state.used)
                local changed = (lastUsed > state.last) or (used > state.used)
                if reset then
                    state.last = lastUsed
                    state.used = used
                elseif changed then
                    if now - (state.lastTrigger or 0) >= 0.12 then
                        state.lastTrigger = now
                        TURNsakenArmKillerHitbox(killer)
                    end
                    state.last = lastUsed
                    state.used = used
                else
                    state.last = lastUsed
                    state.used = used
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for model in pairs(TURNsakenKillerAbilityState) do
        if not model.Parent then TURNsakenKillerAbilityState[model] = nil end
    end
end

local cachedAnimator = nil
local _nearestKillerCache = nil
local _nearestKillerCacheTime = 0
local _nearestKillerCachePos = Vector3.zero
local function getNearestKillerModel()
    local mr = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not mr then return nil end
    local now = tick()
    if _nearestKillerCache and _nearestKillerCache.Parent and (now - _nearestKillerCacheTime) < 0.1 then
        local cacheHRP = _nearestKillerCache:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if cacheHRP and (cacheHRP.Position - _nearestKillerCachePos).Magnitude < 5 then return _nearestKillerCache end
    end
    local c, d = nil, math.huge
    for _, k in ipairs(KillersFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if k:IsA("Model") then
            local hrp = k:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then local dd = (hrp.Position - mr.Position).Magnitude; if dd < d then c, d = k, dd end end
        end
    end
    if c then
        _nearestKillerCache = c; _nearestKillerCacheTime = now
        local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if hrp then _nearestKillerCachePos = hrp.Position end
    end
    return c
end

function beginDragIntoKiller(km)
    if _hitboxDraggingDebounce then return end
    if not km or not km.Parent then return end
    local ch = lp and lp.Character; if not ch then return end
    local hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return end
    local thrp = km:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not thrp then return end
    _hitboxDraggingDebounce = true
    local playerIsSprinting = false
    if SprintModule then pcall(function() playerIsSprinting = SprintModule.IsSprinting == true end) end
    local currentSpeed
    if autoAdaptSpeed then
        if playerIsSprinting then currentSpeed = DspeedSprint else currentSpeed = DspeedWalk end
    else
        currentSpeed = Dspeed
    end
    local ow, oj, oa = hum.WalkSpeed, hum.JumpPower, hum.AutoRotate
    local startY = hrp.Position.Y
    hum.WalkSpeed = 0; hum.JumpPower = 0; hum.AutoRotate = false
    local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", hrp)
    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5); bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local dst = tick()
    local conn
    conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not _hitboxDraggingDebounce then
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            pcall(function() if bv and bv.Parent then bv:Destroy() end end)
            if hum and hum.Parent then hum.WalkSpeed = ow; hum.JumpPower = oj; hum.AutoRotate = oa end
            return
        end
        if not (ch and ch.Parent) or not (km and km.Parent) then _hitboxDraggingDebounce = false; return end
        thrp = km:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not thrp then _hitboxDraggingDebounce = false; return end
        local el = tick() - dst
        if el >= HDT_Duration then _hitboxDraggingDebounce = false; return end
        local mp = hrp.Position; local tp = thrp.Position
        local toTarget = Vector3.new(tp.X - mp.X, 0, tp.Z - mp.Z); local horizDist = toTarget.Magnitude
        if horizDist > HDT_StopDistance then
            local dir = toTarget.Unit; local speed = currentSpeed
            local accelT = math.clamp(el / 0.15, 0, 1); speed = speed * (0.5 + 0.5 * accelT)
            if horizDist < 8 then
                local distFactor = math.clamp((horizDist - HDT_StopDistance) / (8 - HDT_StopDistance), 0.3, 1)
                speed = speed * distFactor
            end
            bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * speed, 0, dir.Z * speed)
        else
            bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0); _hitboxDraggingDebounce = false; return
        end
        if horizDist > 0.01 then
            local lookAt = CFrame.lookAt(mp, Vector3.new(tp.X, mp.Y, tp.Z))
            local turnAlpha = math.clamp(HDT_TurnSpeed * 0.02, 0.04, 0.2)
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame:Lerp(lookAt, turnAlpha)
        end
        local yd = math.abs(hrp.Position.Y - startY)
        if yd > 1.5 then
            local r = hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, startY, hrp.Position.Z) * r
        end
    end)
    task.delay(HDT_Duration + 0.2, function() if _hitboxDraggingDebounce then _hitboxDraggingDebounce = false end end)
end

local function refreshAnimator()
    local ch = lp.Character; if not ch then cachedAnimator = nil; return end
    local h = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    cachedAnimator = h and h:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
end
refreshAnimator()
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5) refreshAnimator() end)

local function connectAutoPunchNetwork()
    if not NetworkModule then return end
    pcall(function()
        NetworkModule:SetConnection(("%*1337ParryIcon"):format(lp.Name), "REMOTE_EVENT", function(b)
            if b and autoPunchEventEnabled then fireRemotePunch() end
        end)
    end)
end
connectAutoPunchNetwork()
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.9); connectAutoPunchNetwork() end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        if not hitboxDraggingTech or _hitboxDraggingDebounce or not isLocalSurvivor() then continue end
        if not cachedAnimator then refreshAnimator() end
        local a = cachedAnimator
        if not a then continue end
        for _, t in ipairs(a:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            local aid = nil
            pcall(function() aid = t.Animation and tostring(t.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+") end)
            if aid and (blockAnimSet[aid] or blockSetTrigger[aid]) then
                local tp = 0; pcall(function() tp = t.TimePosition or 0 end)
                if tp <= 0.15 then
                    local n = getNearestKillerModel()
                    if n then task.wait(Ddelay); task.spawn(function() beginDragIntoKiller(n) end) end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- Punch Aim (BodyGyro 修复版 - 不锁移动)
-- ============================================================
local punchAimEnabled = false
local punchAimRange = 100
local punchAimDuration = 0.15
local punchAimSmooth = 0.6
local punchAimPredict = true
local punchAimConnection = nil
local punchAimTargetHRP = nil
local punchAimTrack = nil
local punchAimStartTime = 0
local punchAimEndTime = 0
local punchAimBodyGyro = nil

local function getClosestTargetForPunch(md)
    local ch = lp.Character; if not ch then return nil end
    local mr = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not mr then return nil end
    local ik = ch.Parent == KillersFolder; local tf = ik and SurvivorsFolder or KillersFolder
    if not tf then return nil end
    local b, bd = nil, md
    for _, m in ipairs(tf:GetChildren()) do
        if m:IsA("Model") and m ~= ch then
            local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); local r = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if h and r and h.Health > 0 then
                local d = (r.Position - mr.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bd then bd = d; b = r end
            end
        end
    end
    return b
end
local function getPredictLead(v, d)
    local h = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z); local s = h.Magnitude
    local p = 0.05; pcall(function() p = lp:GetNetworkPing() end); p = math.clamp(p, 0.02, 0.3)
    local dt = (d or 50) / 500
    local pt = math.clamp(p + 0.06 + dt, 0.06, 0.35)
    local sf = 1 + math.clamp(s / 80, 0, 0.4)
    return h * pt * sf
end
local function releasePunchAim()
    punchAimTargetHRP = nil
    punchAimTrack = nil
    punchAimEndTime = 0
    if punchAimBodyGyro then
        pcall(function() punchAimBodyGyro:Destroy() end)
        punchAimBodyGyro = nil
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        if not punchAimEnabled or not punchAimTargetHRP or not punchAimTargetHRP.Parent then continue end
        if punchAimTrack and not punchAimTrack.IsPlaying then releasePunchAim(); continue end
        if tick() > punchAimEndTime then releasePunchAim(); continue end
        local ch = lp.Character; if not ch then releasePunchAim(); continue end
        local hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then releasePunchAim(); continue end
        local mp = hrp.Position; local tp = punchAimTargetHRP.Position; local d = (tp - mp).Magnitude
        local lead = punchAimPredict and getPredictLead(punchAimTargetHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity, d) or Vector3.zero
        local pp = tp + lead; local af = Vector3.new(pp.X, mp.Y, pp.Z); local tcf = CFrame.lookAt(mp, af)

        -- 使用 BodyGyro 只控制转向，不影响移动
        if not punchAimBodyGyro or not punchAimBodyGyro.Parent then
            punchAimBodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            punchAimBodyGyro.P = 100000
            punchAimBodyGyro.D = 1000
            punchAimBodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0) -- 只转Y轴
            punchAimBodyGyro.Parent = hrp
        end
        punchAimBodyGyro.CFrame = tcf
    end
end)

local function setupPunchAimListener(ch)
    if punchAimConnection then punchAimConnection:Disconnect(); punchAimConnection = nil end
    if not ch then return end
    local h = ch:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5); if not h then return end
    local a = h:WaitForChild("Animator", 5); if not a then return end
    punchAimConnection = a.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(t)
        if not punchAimEnabled then return end
        local aid = t.Animation and t.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")
        if aid and PUNCH_ANIM_IDS[aid] then
            local th = getClosestTargetForPunch(punchAimRange)
            if th then punchAimTargetHRP = th; punchAimTrack = t; punchAimStartTime = tick(); punchAimEndTime = tick() + punchAimDuration end
        end
    end)
end
local function onCharAddedPunch(c) task.wait(0.5); setupPunchAimListener(c) end
if lp.Character then setupPunchAimListener(lp.Character) end
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharAddedPunch)

-- ============================================================
-- [自动发电机 2.0]
-- ============================================================
local Forsaken = {RoundGenerators = {}}
local TURNsakenAutoGeneratorFarm = false
local TURNsakenInstantlyEnterGenerator = false

local MinigameSolver = { Enabled = false, ManualEnabled = false, AutoEnabled = false, Generation = 0, LockBoard = nil, Installed = false }
local GenConfig = { StartDelay = 1.5, ActionDelay = 0.05, ConnectDelay = 1.0, GearDelay = 0.03 }

local FlowBoardModule, LockBoardModule
local FlowInputModule, GearInputModule, LockInputModule, ConnectInputModule
local ModulesReady = false

do
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local m = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        local mg = m and m:FindFirstChild("Minigames")
        local fb = mg and mg:FindFirstChild("FlowBoard")
        local lb = mg and mg:FindFirstChild("LockBoard")
        if fb then FlowBoardModule = require(fb) end
        if lb then LockBoardModule = require(lb) end
        local s = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Systems")
        local p = s and s:FindFirstChild("Player")
        local g = p and p:FindFirstChild("Game")
        local mf = g and g:FindFirstChild("Minigames")
        if mf then
            local f = mf:FindFirstChild("FlowClientMinigame")
            local ge = mf:FindFirstChild("GearClientMinigame")
            local l = mf:FindFirstChild("LockClientMinigame")
            local c = mf:FindFirstChild("ConnectClientMinigame")
            local fi = f and f:FindFirstChild("FlowInput")
            local gi = ge and ge:FindFirstChild("GearInput")
            local li = l and l:FindFirstChild("LockInput")
            local ci = c and c:FindFirstChild("ConnectInput")
            if fi then FlowInputModule = require(fi) end
            if gi then GearInputModule = require(gi) end
            if li then LockInputModule = require(li) end
            if ci then ConnectInputModule = require(ci) end
        end
        ModulesReady = FlowBoardModule and FlowInputModule and GearInputModule and LockInputModule and ConnectInputModule
    end)
    if ok and ModulesReady then print("[TURNsaken] Minigame modules loaded") end
end

local function solverDelay() return math.max(0, GenConfig.ActionDelay or 0.05) end
local function connectSolverDelay() return math.max(0, GenConfig.ConnectDelay or 1) end
local function solverIsEnabled() return MinigameSolver.ManualEnabled or MinigameSolver.AutoEnabled end
local function stillSolving(_, object) return solverIsEnabled() and object and not object.Ended end
local function waitForSolver(object)
    while object and not object.Ended and not solverIsEnabled() do task.wait(0.05) end
    if not (object and not object.Ended and solverIsEnabled()) then return false end
    local started = os.clock()
    while object and not object.Ended and solverIsEnabled() and os.clock() - started < GenConfig.StartDelay do task.wait(0.05) end
    return object and not object.Ended and solverIsEnabled()
end

local function solveFlowBoard(board)
    local size, pairMap = board.Size, board.Pairs
    local occupied, answer = {}, {}
    local directions = { {-1, 0}, {1, 0}, {0, -1}, {0, 1} }
    local endpointOwner = {}
    for color, pair in pairs(pairMap) do endpointOwner[pair[1]], endpointOwner[pair[2]] = color, color end
    local remaining = {}
    for color in pairs(pairMap) do table.insert(remaining, color) end
    local function candidates(color, limit)
        local pair = pairMap[color]
        local from, target = pair[1], pair[2]
        local results, path, seen = {}, {from}, {[from] = true}
        local function distance(cell)
            local r, c = FlowBoardModule.ToRowCol(size, cell)
            local tr, tc = FlowBoardModule.ToRowCol(size, target)
            return math.abs(r - tr) + math.abs(c - tc)
        end
        local function walk(cell)
            if #results >= limit then return end
            if cell == target then table.insert(results, table.clone(path)); return end
            local row, col = FlowBoardModule.ToRowCol(size, cell)
            local nextCells = {}
            for _, d in ipairs(directions) do
                local nr, nc = row + d[1], col + d[2]
                if nr >= 1 and nr <= size and nc >= 1 and nc <= size then
                    local n = FlowBoardModule.ToIndex(size, nr, nc)
                    local owner = endpointOwner[n]
                    if not seen[n] and not occupied[n] and (not owner or (owner == color and n == target)) then table.insert(nextCells, n) end
                end
            end
            table.sort(nextCells, function(a, b) return distance(a) < distance(b) end)
            for _, n in ipairs(nextCells) do
                seen[n] = true; table.insert(path, n)
                walk(n)
                path[#path] = nil; seen[n] = nil
                if #results >= limit then break end
            end
        end
        walk(from)
        table.sort(results, function(a, b) return #a < #b end)
        return results
    end
    local function search(colors)
        if #colors == 0 then return true end
        local bestIndex, bestPaths
        for i, color in ipairs(colors) do
            local list = candidates(color, 300)
            if #list == 0 then return false end
            if not bestPaths or #list < #bestPaths then bestIndex, bestPaths = i, list end
        end
        local color = colors[bestIndex]
        local nextColors = table.clone(colors)
        table.remove(nextColors, bestIndex)
        for _, path in ipairs(bestPaths) do
            for _, cell in ipairs(path) do occupied[cell] = true end
            answer[color] = path
            if search(nextColors) then return true end
            answer[color] = nil
            for _, cell in ipairs(path) do occupied[cell] = nil end
        end
        return false
    end
    return search(remaining) and answer or nil
end

local function installMinigameHooks()
    if MinigameSolver.Installed or not ModulesReady then return end
    MinigameSolver.Installed = true
    pcall(function()
        local oldLockBoardNew = LockBoardModule.new
        LockBoardModule.new = function(...) local board = oldLockBoardNew(...); MinigameSolver.LockBoard = board; return board end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local oldFlowNew = FlowInputModule.new
        FlowInputModule.new = function(board, renderer, callbacks)
            local object = oldFlowNew(board, renderer, callbacks); local token = MinigameSolver.Generation
            task.spawn(function()
                if not waitForSolver(object) then return end
                task.wait(solverDelay())
                local paths = solveFlowBoard(board)
                if not paths then return end
                for color, path in pairs(paths) do
                    if not stillSolving(token, object) then return end
                    local drawing = board:Begin(path[1]); renderer:Render(); task.wait(solverDelay())
                    for index = 2, #path do
                        if not stillSolving(token, object) then return end
                        board:Step(drawing, path[index], 0); renderer:Render(); task.wait(solverDelay())
                    end
                    board:Release(drawing, true); renderer:Render(); task.wait(solverDelay())
                end
                if stillSolving(token, object) and board:IsSolved() then object:__finish(); callbacks.OnSolved() end
            end)
            return object
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local oldConnectNew = ConnectInputModule.new
        ConnectInputModule.new = function(board, renderer, callbacks)
            local object = oldConnectNew(board, renderer, callbacks); local token = MinigameSolver.Generation
            task.spawn(function()
                if not waitForSolver(object) then return end
                task.wait(connectSolverDelay())
                for blob in pairs(board.Blobs) do
                    if not stillSolving(token, object) then return end
                    if not board:GetLink(blob) then
                        local mate = board:GetMate(blob)
                        if mate then board:Connect(blob, mate); renderer:Render(); task.wait(connectSolverDelay()) end
                    end
                end
                if stillSolving(token, object) and board:IsSolved() then object:__finish(); callbacks.OnSolved() end
            end)
            return object
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local oldGearNew = GearInputModule.new
        GearInputModule.new = function(getCenter, callbacks, enabled)
            local object = oldGearNew(getCenter, callbacks, enabled); local token = MinigameSolver.Generation
            task.spawn(function()
                if not waitForSolver(object) then return end
                task.wait(math.max(0.55, solverDelay()))
                for _ = 1, 100 do
                    if not stillSolving(token, object) then return end
                    callbacks.OnTurn(90); task.wait(math.max(0, GenConfig.GearDelay or 0.03))
                end
            end)
            return object
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local oldLockNew = LockInputModule.new
        LockInputModule.new = function(ui, callbacks)
            local object = oldLockNew(ui, callbacks); local board = MinigameSolver.LockBoard; local token = MinigameSolver.Generation
            task.spawn(function()
                if not waitForSolver(object) then return end
                while stillSolving(token, object) and board and not board:IsBroken() do
                    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                    if board:IsOnTarget(board:AngleAt(now)) then callbacks.OnPress(); task.wait(math.max(0.08, solverDelay()))
                    else RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
                end
            end)
            return object
        end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function() task.wait(1); pcall(installMinigameHooks) end)

local function GetGameMap()
    local m = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map'); local ig = m and m:FindFirstChild('Ingame')
    return ig and ig:FindFirstChild('Map')
end
local function UpdateGenerators()
    local gm = GetGameMap(); if not gm then Forsaken.RoundGenerators = {}; return end
    local g = {}
    for _, o in ipairs(gm:GetChildren()) do if o.Name == "Generator" then table.insert(g, o) end end
    Forsaken.RoundGenerators = g
end
task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(0.5); UpdateGenerators() end end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.2)
        for _, g in ipairs(Forsaken.RoundGenerators) do
            local m = g:FindFirstChild("Main"); local p = m and m:FindFirstChild("Prompt")
            if p then p.HoldDuration = TURNsakenInstantlyEnterGenerator and 0 or 0.25 end
        end
    end
end)

local CompletingAll = false
local function CompleteGenerators()
    if CompletingAll or not lp.Character then return end
    CompletingAll = true
    task.spawn(function()
        for _, g in ipairs(Forsaken.RoundGenerators) do
            local p = g:FindFirstChild("Progress")
            if not p or p.Value == 100 then continue end
            local m = g:FindFirstChild("Main"); local pr = m and m:FindFirstChild("Prompt")
            if not pr then continue end
            local pos = g:FindFirstChild("Positions"); if not pos then continue end
            lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = pos.Center.CFrame
            lp.Character.Humanoid:MoveTo(m.Position)
            local t0 = tick()
            repeat fireproximityprompt(pr); task.wait(0.5) until tick() - t0 >= 7 or lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("PuzzleUI")
            if tick() - t0 >= 7 then continue end
            task.wait(0.4)
            MinigameSolver.AutoEnabled = true; MinigameSolver.Generation += 1
            repeat task.wait() until (not g:FindFirstChild("Progress")) or g.Progress.Value == 100 or (not lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("PuzzleUI"))
            MinigameSolver.AutoEnabled = false
        end
        CompletingAll = false
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- ESP 核心
-- ============================================================
local espEnabled = {Generator=false, Killer=false, Survivor=false, Item=false, Gear=false, Cooldown=false, OutlineOnly=false, NoOutline=false}
local espColors = {Generator=Color3.fromRGB(221,51,255), Killer=Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0), Survivor=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,255), Item=Color3.fromRGB(0,255,255), Gear=Color3.fromRGB(255,165,0)}
local espCache = {}

local function RemoveESP(m)
    local d = espCache[m]
    if d then
        pcall(function() if d.hl then d.hl:Destroy() end end)
        pcall(function() if d.bb then d.bb:Destroy() end end)
        espCache[m] = nil
    end
end

local function CreateESP(m, c, tf, customRoot)
    if not m or not m.Parent then return nil end
    local cached = espCache[m]
    if cached then
        if cached.hl then cached.hl.FillColor = c; cached.hl.OutlineColor = c end
        return cached
    end
    local ok, d = pcall(function()
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Name = "TURNsaken_ESP_HL"; hl.Adornee = m
        hl.FillColor = c; hl.OutlineColor = c
        hl.FillTransparency = espEnabled.OutlineOnly and 1 or 0.7
        hl.OutlineTransparency = espEnabled.NoOutline and 1 or 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop; hl.Parent = m
        local r = customRoot or m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or m:FindFirstChild("ItemRoot")
            or m:FindFirstChild("Main")
            or m.PrimaryPart
            or (m:IsA("BasePart") and m)
            or m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        local bb, lbl = nil, nil
        if r then
            bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "TURNsaken_ESP_BB"; bb.Adornee = r
            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 30); bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true; bb.Parent = r
            lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.TextColor3 = c; lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0); lbl.TextSize = 12
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        end
        return {hl = hl, bb = bb, lbl = lbl, textFunc = tf, color = c, root = r}
    end)
    if ok and d then espCache[m] = d; return d end
    return nil
end

local gearCache = {}
local function isGearPrompt(prompt)
    if not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return false end
    local action = (prompt.ActionText or ""):lower()
    local obj = (prompt.ObjectText or ""):lower()
    return action:find("gear") or obj:find("gear") or action:find("齿轮") or obj:find("齿轮")
end

local function registerGear(prompt)
    if not isGearPrompt(prompt) then return end
    local part = prompt.Parent
    if part and part:IsA("Attachment") then part = part.Parent end
    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
        local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or part
        gearCache[model] = {part = part, prompt = prompt}
    end
end

workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
    if inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then task.defer(registerGear, inst) end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    for _, inst in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then registerGear(inst) end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.8)
        pcall(function()
            for m, _ in pairs(espCache) do if not m or not m.Parent then RemoveESP(m) end end
        end)
        local am = {}

        if espEnabled.Generator then
            pcall(function()
                for _, g in ipairs(Forsaken.RoundGenerators) do
                    if g and g.Parent and g:FindFirstChild("Main") then
                        am[g] = {color = espColors.Generator, text = function()
                            local p = g:FindFirstChild("Progress") and g.Progress.Value or 0
                            return string.format("Generator %d%%", p)
                        end}
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local pf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
        if pf then
            local kf = pf:FindFirstChild("Killers"); local sf = pf:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
            if kf and espEnabled.Killer then
                pcall(function()
                    for _, m in ipairs(kf:GetChildren()) do
                        if m:IsA("Model") and m ~= lp.Character then
                            am[m] = {color = espColors.Killer, text = function()
                                local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                return m.Name .. (h and string.format(" HP:%d", math.floor(h.Health)) or "")
                            end}
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if sf and espEnabled.Survivor then
                pcall(function()
                    for _, m in ipairs(sf:GetChildren()) do
                        if m:IsA("Model") and m ~= lp.Character then
                            am[m] = {color = espColors.Survivor, text = function()
                                local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                return m.Name .. (h and string.format(" HP:%d", math.floor(h.Health)) or "")
                            end}
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        if espEnabled.Item then
            pcall(function()
                local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map"); local seen = {}
                local function check(o)
                    if seen[o] then return end
                    if not (o:IsA("Model") or o:IsA("Tool")) then return end
                    local n = o.Name:lower()
                    if n:find("medkit") or n:find("bloxy") or n:find("cola") then
                        seen[o] = true
                        am[o] = {color = espColors.Item, text = function()
                            local nn = o.Name:lower()
                            if nn:find("medkit") then return "Medkit (医疗包)"
                            elseif nn:find("bloxy") or nn:find("cola") then return "Cola (可乐)"
                            else return o.Name end
                        end}
                    end
                end
                if map then for _, o in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do check(o) end end
                for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do check(o) end
            end)
        end

        if espEnabled.Gear then
            pcall(function()
                for model, data in pairs(gearCache) do
                    if not model.Parent or not data.part.Parent then
                        gearCache[model] = nil
                        RemoveESP(model)
                    else
                        am[model] = {color = espColors.Gear, text = function() return "Gear (齿轮)" end, customRoot = data.part}
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        pcall(function()
            for m, i in pairs(am) do
                local d = CreateESP(m, i.color, i.text, i.customRoot)
                if d then
                    if d.hl then
                        d.hl.FillColor = i.color; d.hl.OutlineColor = i.color
                        d.hl.FillTransparency = espEnabled.OutlineOnly and 1 or 0.7
                        d.hl.OutlineTransparency = espEnabled.NoOutline and 1 or 0
                    end
                    if d.lbl then d.lbl.TextColor3 = i.color; pcall(function() d.lbl.Text = i.text() end) end
                end
            end
            for m, _ in pairs(espCache) do if not am[m] then RemoveESP(m) end end
        end)
    end
end)

local skillAnimMap = {["72722244508749"]={Name="Block (格挡)", CD=8}, ["96959123077498"]={Name="Slash (斩击)", CD=1}}
local cooldownCache = {}
local function ShowCooldownOnKiller(km, sn, cd)
    if not km or not km.Parent then return end
    local r = km:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not r then return end
    local ck = km.Name .. "_" .. sn
    if cooldownCache[ck] then cooldownCache[ck].endTime = tick() + cd; return end
    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    bb.Name = "TURNsaken_CD_BB"; bb.Adornee = r
    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 40); bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true; bb.Parent = r
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,0); lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0); lbl.TextSize = 14; lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    lbl.Text = sn .. " CD: " .. string.format("%.1f", cd) .. "s"
    cooldownCache[ck] = {bb = bb, lbl = lbl, endTime = tick() + cd}
end
local function TrackKillerCooldown(km)
    if not km or not km:IsA("Model") then return end
    local h = km:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5); if not h then return end
    local a = h:WaitForChild("Animator", 5); if not a then return end
    a.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(t)
        if not espEnabled.Cooldown then return end
        local aid = t.Animation and tostring(t.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+")
        if aid and skillAnimMap[aid] then ShowCooldownOnKiller(km, skillAnimMap[aid].Name, skillAnimMap[aid].CD) end
    end)
end
KillersFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(k) if k:IsA("Model") and k:GetAttribute("Username") then TrackKillerCooldown(k) end end)
for _, k in ipairs(KillersFolder:GetChildren()) do if k:IsA("Model") and k:GetAttribute("Username") then TrackKillerCooldown(k) end end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        if espEnabled.Cooldown then
            for k, d in pairs(cooldownCache) do
                if d.bb and d.bb.Parent then
                    local rm = d.endTime - tick()
                    if rm <= 0 then pcall(function() d.bb:Destroy() end); cooldownCache[k] = nil
                    else d.lbl.Text = d.lbl.Text:gsub("CD: [%d%.]+s", "CD: " .. string.format("%.1f", rm) .. "s") end
                else cooldownCache[k] = nil end
            end
        end
    end
end)

end)

    local old = GetMousePos
    GetMousePos = function(...)
        if silentAimEnabled then
            local c = getNearestKillerModel()
            if c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return c.HumanoidRootPart.Position end
        end
        return old(...)
    end
end

local voidRushAntiCrash = false
local demonicPursuitAntiCrash = false
local voidRushControl = false
local janeDoeInstantCharge = false
local voidRushTracker = 0
local pursuitTracker = 0
if NetworkModule then
    pcall(function()
        local old = NetworkModule.FireServerConnection
        NetworkModule.FireServerConnection = function(self, ...)
            local a = {...}
            if voidRushAntiCrash and a[1] == (lp.Name .. "VoidRushCollision") then repeat task.wait() until tick() - voidRushTracker >= 0.8 end
            if demonicPursuitAntiCrash and a[1] == (lp.Name .. "666Crashed") then repeat task.wait() until tick() - pursuitTracker >= 1.5 end
            return old(self, ...)
        end
    end)
end

        for _, k in ipairs(KillersFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if k:IsA("Model") then
                if k:GetAttribute("VoidRushState") == "Dashing" then voidRushTracker = tick() end
                if k:GetAttribute("PursuitState") == "Dashing" then pursuitTracker = tick() end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local autoRagingPace = false
local auto404Error = false
local autoParryRange = 15
local autoParryDelay = 0
local lastRagingPaceTime = 0
local last404Time = 0
local validAttackAnims = {}
for _, id in ipairs(punchAnimIds) do validAttackAnims[id] = true end
for _, id in ipairs({"119434518007321","115194624791339","89448354637442","100725497418533","107640065977686","112902284724598","106086955212611","77119710693654","73921036900313","111384272984267","90499469533503","133491532453922"}) do validAttackAnims[id] = true end
local function isLocalKillerByName(n)
    local c = lp.Character
    return c and c.Name:find(n) and c.Parent == KillersFolder
end
local function getAbilityCD(n)
    local mu = lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainUI"); local ac = mu and mu:FindFirstChild("AbilityContainer")
    local b = ac and ac:FindFirstChild(n)
    if b then local cd = b:FindFirstChild("CooldownTime"); if cd and cd.Text ~= "" then return tonumber(cd.Text) or 0 end end
    return 0
end
local function checkNearbySurvivorsAttacking()
    local mr = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not mr then return false end
    for _, s in ipairs(SurvivorsFolder:GetChildren()) do
        local sr = s:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if sr and (sr.Position - mr.Position).Magnitude <= autoParryRange then
            local h = s:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); local a = h and h:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if a then
                for _, t in ipairs(a:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    local aid = tostring(t.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+")
                    if aid and validAttackAnims[aid] and t.TimePosition < 0.45 then return true end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        if autoRagingPace and isLocalKillerByName("Slasher") then
            if getAbilityCD("RagingPace") <= 0 and tick() - lastRagingPaceTime >= 2 then
                if checkNearbySurvivorsAttacking() then
                    if autoParryDelay > 0 then task.wait(autoParryDelay) end
                    lastRagingPaceTime = tick()
                    if NetworkModule then pcall(function() NetworkModule:FireServerConnection("UseActorAbility", "REMOTE_EVENT", "RagingPace") end) end
                end
            end
        end
        if auto404Error and isLocalKillerByName("JohnDoe") then
            if getAbilityCD("404Error") <= 0 and tick() - last404Time >= 2 then
                if checkNearbySurvivorsAttacking() then
                    if autoParryDelay > 0 then task.wait(autoParryDelay) end
                    last404Time = tick()
                    if NetworkModule then pcall(function() NetworkModule:FireServerConnection("UseActorAbility", "REMOTE_EVENT", "404Error") end) end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local autoPickupNearItems = false

    while true do
        task.wait(1.0)
        if autoPickupNearItems and lp.Character then
            pcall(function()
                local r = lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if r then
                    local parts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(r.Position, 20)
                    for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
                        local pr = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                        if pr then
                            local pn = p.Name:lower(); local ppn = p.Parent and p.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
                            local isMatch = pn:find("medkit") or pn:find("bloxy") or pn:find("cola")
                                or ppn:find("medkit") or ppn:find("bloxy") or ppn:find("cola")
                            if isMatch then pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(pr) end) end
                        end
                    end
                    for model, data in pairs(gearCache) do
                        if data.part and data.part.Parent then
                            if (data.part.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
                                pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(data.prompt) end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

local antiSubspace = false
local antiBlindness = false
local antiGlitched = false
local antiHallucinate = false
local anti007Clone = false
local antiBlindForce = false
local originalHooks = {}
local function hookEffect(m, fg)
    if not m then return end
    local k = tostring(m)
    if originalHooks[k] then return end
    pcall(function()
        if not m.Applied then return end
        local orig
        orig = hookfunction(m.Applied, newcclosure(function(...)
            if fg() then return end
            return orig(...)
        end))
        originalHooks[k] = orig
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(3)
    if Effects_Subspace then hookEffect(Effects_Subspace, function() return antiSubspace end) end
    if Effects_Blindness then hookEffect(Effects_Blindness, function() return antiBlindness end) end
    if Effects_Glitched then hookEffect(Effects_Glitched, function() return antiGlitched end) end
    if Effects_Stunned then hookEffect(Effects_Stunned, function() return antiHallucinate end) end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        if antiBlindForce then
            pcall(function()
                for _, e in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or e:IsA("BloomEffect") then e.Enabled = false end
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                local c = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if c then
                    for _, e in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                        if e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or e:IsA("BloomEffect") then e.Enabled = false end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(1.0)
        if anti007Clone then
            local m = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map"); local ig = m and m:FindFirstChild("Ingame")
            if ig then
                for _, o in ipairs(ig:GetChildren()) do
                    if o.Name == "007n7" and o:GetAttribute("Username") ~= lp.Name then o:Destroy() end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local autoRemovePopups = false
local autoEscapeNosferatu = false
local autoNosferatuReel = false
local walkOverFootprint = false
local walkOverTrail = false
local walkOverPuddle = false
local showChat = false
local privacyBypass = false
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.5)
        if autoRemovePopups then
            local t = lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TemporaryUI")
            if t then for _, o in ipairs(t:GetChildren()) do if o.Name == "1x1x1x1Popup" then o:Destroy() end end end
        end
        if showChat then
            local c = TextChatService:FindFirstChildOfClass("ChatWindowConfiguration")
            if c then c.Enabled = true end
        end
    end
end)
local function NeedToEscapeOrReel()
    local u = lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TemporaryUI"); if not u then return false end
    local q = u:FindFirstChild("QTE")
    return q ~= nil and q:FindFirstChild('Zone3') == nil
end
local function onTempUI(child)
    if not (autoEscapeNosferatu or autoNosferatuReel) then return end
    if child.Name == "QTE" then
        task.spawn(function()
            if NeedToEscapeOrReel() then
                local ik = KillersFolder:FindFirstChild(lp.Name) ~= nil
                if (ik and autoNosferatuReel) or (not ik and autoEscapeNosferatu) then
                    local kn = ""
                    for _, k in ipairs(KillersFolder:GetChildren()) do if k:GetAttribute("Username") then kn = k:GetAttribute("Username"); break end end
                    local a = (ik and lp.Name or kn) .. "NosHookQTE"
                    if NetworkModule then NetworkModule:FireServerConnection(a, "REMOTE_EVENT", true) end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    local t = lp.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("TemporaryUI", 10)
    if t then t.ChildAdded:Connect(onTempUI) end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(1.0)
        if walkOverFootprint or walkOverTrail or walkOverPuddle then
            local m = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map"); local ig = m and m:FindFirstChild("Ingame")
            if ig then
                for _, o in ipairs(ig:GetChildren()) do
                    if walkOverFootprint and o.Name:find("Shadows") then
                        for _, p in ipairs(o:GetChildren()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.CanTouch = false end end
                    end
                    if walkOverTrail and o.Name:find("JohnDoeTrail") then
                        for _, p in ipairs(o:GetChildren()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.CanTouch = false end end
                    end
                    if walkOverPuddle and o.Name:find("Puddle") then
                        if o:IsA("BasePart") then o.CanTouch = false end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
local function ApplyPrivacyBypass(st)
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local pd = p:FindFirstChild("PlayerData"); local s = pd and pd:FindFirstChild("Settings")
        local pv = s and s:FindFirstChild("Privacy")
        if pv then
            for _, k in ipairs({"HideKillerWins","HidePlaytime","HideSurvivorWins"}) do
                local v = pv:FindFirstChild(k)
                if v then
                    if st then
                        if not v:GetAttribute("OriginalValue") then v:SetAttribute("OriginalValue", v.Value) end
                        v.Value = false
                    else
                        local o = v:GetAttribute("OriginalValue")
                        if o ~= nil then v.Value = o end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(5); if privacyBypass then ApplyPrivacyBypass(true) end end end)

local infiniteStamina = false

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.25)
        if infiniteStamina and SprintModule then
            pcall(function()
                SprintModule.MaxStamina = math.huge
                SprintModule.Stamina = math.huge
                if SprintModule.StaminaLoss ~= nil then SprintModule.StaminaLoss = 0 end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

local sceneBrightness = 2
local sceneRemoveFog = false
local sceneNoShadows = false
local sceneFOV = 70

_G._turnsakenCinematicOn = false

local FovSetting = nil
do
    local ok, pd = pcall(function()
        return lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerData") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerData", 8)
    end)
    if ok and pd then
        local ok2, fov = pcall(function()
            return pd:FindFirstChild("FieldOfView", true) or pd:FindFirstChild("FOV", true)
        end)
        if ok2 and fov and fov:IsA("NumberValue") then
            FovSetting = fov
        end
    end
end

local function applyFOV(fov)
    sceneFOV = fov
    if FovSetting then pcall(function() FovSetting.Value = fov end) end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then pcall(function() cam.FieldOfView = fov end) end
end

local function applySceneSettings()
    if _G._turnsakenCinematicOn then return end
    if Lighting.Brightness ~= sceneBrightness then Lighting.Brightness = sceneBrightness end
    if sceneRemoveFog then
        if Lighting.FogEnd ~= math.huge then Lighting.FogEnd = math.huge end
        local a = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
        if a and a.Density ~= 0 then a.Density = 0 end
    else
        if Lighting.FogEnd ~= 100000 then Lighting.FogEnd = 100000 end
        local a = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
        if a and a.Density ~= 0.3 then a.Density = 0.3 end
    end
    local wantShadows = not sceneNoShadows
    if Lighting.GlobalShadows ~= wantShadows then Lighting.GlobalShadows = wantShadows end
end

task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(2); applySceneSettings() end end)

local _fovFrameCounter = 0
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        _fovFrameCounter = _fovFrameCounter + 1
        if _fovFrameCounter < 15 then continue end
        _fovFrameCounter = 0
        if FovSetting and FovSetting.Value ~= sceneFOV then pcall(function() FovSetting.Value = sceneFOV end) end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam and cam.FieldOfView ~= sceneFOV then pcall(function() cam.FieldOfView = sceneFOV end) end
    end
end)

local _cinematicOriginalState = nil

local function _saveCinematicOriginal()
    if _cinematicOriginalState then return end
    _cinematicOriginalState = {
        Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
        OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
        Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
        ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
        GeographicLatitude = Lighting.GeographicLatitude,
        FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
        FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
        FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
        GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
        EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
        EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
    }
end

local function _destroyCinematicEffects()
    for _, e in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if e.Name == "TURNsakenCinematicCC" or e.Name == "TURNsakenCinematicBloom" or e.Name == "TURNsakenCinematicAtm" then
            pcall(function() e:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    for _, e in ipairs(workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
        if e.Name == "TURNsakenCinematicDOF" then
            pcall(function() e:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

local function applyCinematicLighting()
    _saveCinematicOriginal()
    _destroyCinematicEffects()
    Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(58, 42, 32)
    Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(80, 62, 48)
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0.5
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0.25
    Lighting.Brightness = 2.5
    Lighting.ClockTime = 17.5
    Lighting.GeographicLatitude = 0
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
    Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(120, 85, 55)
    Lighting.FogStart = 60
    Lighting.FogEnd = 1200
    local atm = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
    if not atm then atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere"); atm.Parent = Lighting end
    atm.Name = "TURNsakenCinematicAtm"
    pcall(function()
        atm.Density = 0.25; atm.Offset = 0.1
        atm.Color = Color3.fromRGB(215, 180, 140)
        atm.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(150, 110, 80)
        atm.Glare = 0.15; atm.Haze = 1.2
    end)
    local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
    cc.Name = "TURNsakenCinematicCC"
    cc.Brightness = 0.02; cc.Contrast = 0.22; cc.Saturation = 0.15
    cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 185)
    cc.Parent = Lighting
    local bloom = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
    bloom.Name = "TURNsakenCinematicBloom"
    bloom.Intensity = 0.85; bloom.Size = 24; bloom.Threshold = 0.92
    bloom.Parent = Lighting
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        local dof = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
        dof.Name = "TURNsakenCinematicDOF"
        dof.FarIntensity = 0.12; dof.FocusDistance = 40; dof.InFocusRadius = 30; dof.NearIntensity = 0
        dof.Parent = cam
    end
end

local function removeCinematicLighting()
    _destroyCinematicEffects()
    if _cinematicOriginalState then
        pcall(function()
            Lighting.Ambient = _cinematicOriginalState.Ambient
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = _cinematicOriginalState.OutdoorAmbient
            Lighting.Brightness = _cinematicOriginalState.Brightness
            Lighting.ClockTime = _cinematicOriginalState.ClockTime
            Lighting.GeographicLatitude = _cinematicOriginalState.GeographicLatitude
            Lighting.FogColor = _cinematicOriginalState.FogColor
            Lighting.FogStart = _cinematicOriginalState.FogStart
            Lighting.FogEnd = _cinematicOriginalState.FogEnd
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = _cinematicOriginalState.GlobalShadows
            Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = _cinematicOriginalState.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
            Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = _cinematicOriginalState.EnvironmentSpecularScale
        end)
    end
end

local russianRatioEnabled = false
local russianRatioAmount = 0.85
local cleanCamCFrame = nil

RunService:BindToRenderStep("TURNsaken_RussianSave", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then cleanCamCFrame = cam.CFrame end
end)

RunService:BindToRenderStep("TURNsaken_RussianApply", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
    if not russianRatioEnabled then return end
    if not cleanCamCFrame then return end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local s = russianRatioAmount
    local scaleMat = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, s, 0, 0, 0, 1)
    cam.CFrame = cleanCamCFrame * scaleMat
end)


-- Menu toggle (Obsidian handles its own keybind via F1)
_G.TURNsakenToggleUI = function()
    if Window and Window.Toggle then pcall(function() Window:Toggle() end) end
end

-- ============================================================
-- Native Killers Batch 1: Anti-Backstab + Auto Dodge
-- (ported from NergPlus, no external backend / no hidden UI)
-- ============================================================
function TURNsakenLoadKillerBatch1()
    local runService = RunService
    local workspaceService = Workspace
    local replicatedStorage = ReplicatedStorage
    local tweenService = TweenService
    local players = Players
    local localPlayer = lp

    local v2data = {}
    local v2 = {
        get = function(key, default)
            local value = v2data[key]
            if value == nil then return default end
            return value
        end,
        set = function(key, value)
            v2data[key] = value
        end,
    }

    local __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE = true
    _G.__NERG_KILLED = false

    local a724RotLocks = {}
    local a724RotLocked = false
    local function a724RotLock(hum, tag)
        a724RotLocks[tag or "default"] = true
        a724RotLocked = true
        hum = hum or (localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
        if hum and hum.Parent then hum.AutoRotate = false end
    end
    local function a724RotUnlock(hum, tag)
        if tag then
            a724RotLocks[tag] = nil
        else
            table.clear(a724RotLocks)
        end
        if next(a724RotLocks) ~= nil then return end
        hum = hum or (localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
        if not a724RotLocked and tag ~= nil then return end
        a724RotLocked = false
        if hum and hum.Parent then hum.AutoRotate = true end
    end

    -- Stop a previous batch instance before re-binding (script re-execution).
    if _G.NERG_ABS and _G.NERG_ABS.stop then pcall(_G.NERG_ABS.stop) end
    if _G.NERG_DODGE and _G.NERG_DODGE.stop then pcall(_G.NERG_DODGE.stop) end

-- [A747] Anti Backstab (port) — event-driven · default OFF · full cleanup
do
  local ABS_SOUND_IDS = { ["134932463887703"] = true, ["101489994047160"] = true }
  local ABS_ANIM_IDS = { ["100725497418533"] = "Stab", ["89448354637442"] = "LungeStart" }
  local ABS_MODEL_NAME = "TwoTime"

  local abs = {
    on = v2.get("a747ABOn", false) and true or false,
    showRing = v2.get("a747ABRing", false) and true or false,
    range = math.clamp(tonumber(v2.get("a747ABRange", 27)) or 27, 10, 120),
    duration = math.clamp(tonumber(v2.get("a747ABDur", 0.8)) or 0.8, 0.15, 2),
    locked = false,
    gui = nil,
    hits = 0,
    myCharConn = nil,
    conns = {},
    soundConns = {},
    animConns = {},
    rings = {},
  }

  local absSession
  local scanThread
  local absWatch
  local absTurn
  local absFolder
  local absFolderToken
  local absFolderConns = {}

  local function absIsKiller()
    local ch = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
    local p = ch and ch.Parent
    return p ~= nil and p.Name == "Killers" and ch:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
  end

  local function absTarget(model)
    local pl = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
    local sv = pl and pl:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
    return sv ~= nil and sv:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and model ~= nil
      and model:IsA("Model") and model.Parent == sv
      and (model.Name == ABS_MODEL_NAME or model:GetAttribute("CharacterName") == ABS_MODEL_NAME)
  end

  local function absLive(session)
    return session ~= nil and absSession == session and abs.on
      and __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE and not _G.__NERG_KILLED
      and localPlayer.Character == session.char and absIsKiller()
  end

  local function absTrack(c)
    if c then abs.conns[#abs.conns + 1] = c end
    return c
  end

  local function absDropTargets()
    absFolderToken = nil
    for _, c in ipairs(absFolderConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    table.clear(absFolderConns)
    absFolder = nil
    for _, c in pairs(abs.animConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    for _, c in pairs(abs.soundConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
  end

  local function absDropConns()
    absWatch = nil
    if abs.myCharConn then
      pcall(function() abs.myCharConn:Disconnect() end)
      abs.myCharConn = nil
    end
    for _, c in ipairs(abs.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    table.clear(abs.conns)
    absDropTargets()
  end

  local function absGui()
    if abs.gui and abs.gui.Parent then return abs.gui end
    local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return nil end
    abs.gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    abs.gui.Name = "NergAbsGui"
    abs.gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    abs.gui.DisplayOrder = 5
    abs.gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    abs.gui.Parent = pg
    return abs.gui
  end

  local function absLabel(show)
    pcall(function()
      local g = absGui()
      if not g then return end
      local lbl = g:FindFirstChild("AbsTaunt")
      if not lbl then
        lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Name = "AbsTaunt"
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 560, 0, 50)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -280, 0.34, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = "At least they tried"
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextSize = 34
        lbl.TextTransparency = 1
        lbl.Parent = g
      end
      tweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(show and 0.15 or 0.5), { TextTransparency = show and 0 or 1 }):Play()
    end)
  end

  local function absRing(model)
    if not abs.on or not abs.showRing or not absIsKiller() then return end
    if abs.rings[model] or not model or not model.Parent then return end
    if not players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) then return end
    local hrp = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    local ring = Instance.new("Part")
    ring.Name = "NergAbsRing"
    ring.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
    ring.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, abs.range * 2, abs.range * 2)
    ring.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
    ring.Color = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
    ring.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
    ring.Transparency = 0.5
    ring.Anchored = false
    ring.CanCollide = false
    ring.CanTouch = false
    ring.CanQuery = false
    ring.CastShadow = false
    ring.Massless = true
    ring.Locked = true
    ring.Parent = hrp
    local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    w.Part0 = hrp
    w.Part1 = ring
    w.Parent = ring
    abs.rings[model] = ring
  end

  local function absRingOff(model)
    local r = abs.rings[model]
    if r then pcall(function() r:Destroy() end) end
    abs.rings[model] = nil
  end

  local function absRingsClear()
    for m in pairs(abs.rings) do absRingOff(m) end
  end

  local function absRingsResize()
    for _, r in pairs(abs.rings) do
      if r and r.Parent then r.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, abs.range * 2, abs.range * 2) end
    end
  end

  local function absNearestModel(pos, limit)
    if not pos then return nil end
    local pl = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
    local sv = pl and pl:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
    if not sv then return nil end
    local best, bestD = nil, limit or 14
    for _, model in ipairs(sv:GetChildren()) do
      if absTarget(model) then
        local hrp = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
          local d = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
          if d < bestD then best, bestD = model, d end
        end
      end
    end
    return best
  end

  local function absFindModel(pos)
    if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then return absNearestModel(pos) end
    local ch = localPlayer.Character
    local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return hrp and absNearestModel(hrp.Position, abs.range) or nil
  end

  local absCatalogBuilt = false
  local function absHarvestCatalog()
    if absCatalogBuilt then return end
    absCatalogBuilt = true
    pcall(function()
      local assets = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
      if not assets then return end
      local function extractIds(val, outTbl, tagVal, depth)
        if (depth or 0) > 3 or val == nil then return end
        if type(val) == "string" or type(val) == "number" then
          local num = tostring(val):match("%d+")
          if num then outTbl[num] = tagVal end
        elseif typeof(val) == "Instance" and (val:IsA("Animation") or val:IsA("Sound")) then
          local raw = val:IsA("Animation") and val.AnimationId or val.SoundId
          local num = tostring(raw):match("%d+")
          if num then outTbl[num] = tagVal end
        elseif type(val) == "table" then
          for _, subv in pairs(val) do extractIds(subv, outTbl, tagVal, (depth or 0) + 1) end
        end
      end
      local function harvestCfg(cfgMod)
        if not (cfgMod and cfgMod:IsA("ModuleScript")) then return end
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, cfgMod)
        if not (ok and type(mod) == "table") then return end
        if type(mod.Animations) == "table" then
          for k, v in pairs(mod.Animations) do
            local kl = tostring(k):lower()
            if kl:find("stab") or kl:find("lunge") or kl:find("dagger") then
              extractIds(v, ABS_ANIM_IDS, tostring(k), 0)
            end
          end
        end
        if type(mod.Sounds) == "table" then
          for k, v in pairs(mod.Sounds) do
            local kl = tostring(k):lower()
            if kl:find("stab") or kl:find("lunge") or kl:find("dagger") then
              extractIds(v, ABS_SOUND_IDS, true, 0)
            end
          end
        end
      end
      local surv = assets:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
      local ttBase = surv and surv:FindFirstChild(ABS_MODEL_NAME)
      if ttBase then harvestCfg(ttBase:FindFirstChild("Config")) end
      local skins = assets:FindFirstChild("Skins")
      local sSurv = skins and skins:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
      local ttSkins = sSurv and sSurv:FindFirstChild(ABS_MODEL_NAME)
      if ttSkins then
        for _, sk in ipairs(ttSkins:GetChildren()) do
          harvestCfg(sk:FindFirstChild("Config"))
        end
      end
    end)
  end

  local function absReleaseTurn()
    local turn = absTurn
    absTurn = nil
    if turn then
      if turn.thread and coroutine.running() ~= turn.thread then pcall(task.cancel, turn.thread) end
      pcall(function() a724RotUnlock(turn.hum, "abs") end)
    end
    abs.locked = false
    if abs.gui and abs.gui.Parent then absLabel(false) end
  end

  local function absTrigger(target)
    if not abs.on or abs.locked or not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED then return end
    if not absIsKiller() then return end
    local ch = localPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return end
    local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    local model
    if typeof(target) == "Instance" then
      model = target
    else
      model = absFindModel(target)
    end
    if not absTarget(model) or model == ch then return end
    local ttRoot = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not ttRoot or not ttRoot.Parent then return end
    if (myRoot.Position - ttRoot.Position).Magnitude > abs.range then return end
    local turn = { hum = hum, char = ch }
    absTurn = turn
    abs.locked = true
    abs.hits += 1
    absLabel(true)
    turn.thread = coroutine.create(function()
      pcall(function()
        if hum and hum.Parent then a724RotLock(hum, "abs") end
        local deadline = os.clock() + abs.duration
        local hp0 = hum and hum.Health
        while os.clock() < deadline and abs.on and __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE and not _G.__NERG_KILLED do
          if absTurn ~= turn or localPlayer.Character ~= ch or not absIsKiller() or not absTarget(model) then break end
          local ch2 = localPlayer.Character
          local r2 = ch2 and ch2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
          local hum2 = ch2 and ch2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
          if hum2 ~= hum then break end
          if hp0 and hum2 and hum2.Health < hp0 - 0.5 then break end
          if not r2 or not ttRoot.Parent then break end
          local p2 = r2.Position
          local t2 = ttRoot.Position
          local flat = Vector3.new(t2.X - p2.X, 0, t2.Z - p2.Z)
          if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then break end
          if hum2 then a724RotLock(hum2, "abs") end
          r2.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(p2, Vector3.new(t2.X, p2.Y, t2.Z))
          runService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
      end)
      if absTurn == turn then absReleaseTurn() end
    end)
    task.spawn(turn.thread)
  end

  local function absOwned(model, owner, session)
    return absLive(session) and absTarget(model) and abs.animConns[model] == owner
  end

  local function absCheckSound(snd, model, owner, session)
    if not absOwned(model, owner, session) or not snd:IsDescendantOf(model) then return end
    local ok, id = pcall(function() return snd.SoundId:match("%d+") end)
    if ok and id and ABS_SOUND_IDS[id] then absTrigger(model) end
  end

  local function absBindSound(snd, model, owner, session)
    if not absOwned(model, owner, session) then return end
    if not (snd and snd:IsA("Sound") and snd:IsDescendantOf(model)) or abs.soundConns[snd] then return end
    local cId, cPlay, cPlaying, cAnc
    local entry = { owner = owner }
    local function dropSnd()
      if abs.soundConns[snd] == entry then abs.soundConns[snd] = nil end
      if cId then pcall(function() cId:Disconnect() end) cId = nil end
      if cPlay then pcall(function() cPlay:Disconnect() end) cPlay = nil end
      if cPlaying then pcall(function() cPlaying:Disconnect() end) cPlaying = nil end
      if cAnc then pcall(function() cAnc:Disconnect() end) cAnc = nil end
    end
    entry.Disconnect = dropSnd
    abs.soundConns[snd] = entry
    cId = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SoundId"):Connect(function()
      if snd.IsPlaying then absCheckSound(snd, model, owner, session) end
    end)
    cPlay = snd.Played:Connect(function() absCheckSound(snd, model, owner, session) end)
    cPlaying = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("IsPlaying"):Connect(function()
      if snd.IsPlaying then absCheckSound(snd, model, owner, session) end
    end)
    cAnc = snd.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
      if not snd:IsDescendantOf(model) then dropSnd() end
    end)
    if snd.IsPlaying then absCheckSound(snd, model, owner, session) end
  end

  local function absScanSounds(model, owner, session)
    for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
      if d:IsA("Sound") then absBindSound(d, model, owner, session) end
    end
  end

  local function absHookModel(model)
    local session = absSession
    if not absLive(session) or not absTarget(model) then return end
    if abs.showRing then absRing(model) else absRingOff(model) end
    if abs.animConns[model] then return end
    local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end
    local owner = {}
    local cAnim, cDesc, cAnc
    owner.Disconnect = function()
      if abs.animConns[model] == owner then abs.animConns[model] = nil end
      if cAnim then pcall(function() cAnim:Disconnect() end) cAnim = nil end
      if cDesc then pcall(function() cDesc:Disconnect() end) cDesc = nil end
      if cAnc then pcall(function() cAnc:Disconnect() end) cAnc = nil end
      for _, entry in pairs(abs.soundConns) do
        if entry.owner == owner then entry:Disconnect() end
      end
      absRingOff(model)
    end
    abs.animConns[model] = owner
    cAnim = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
      if not absOwned(model, owner, session) then return end
      local ok, aid = pcall(function() return track.Animation.AnimationId end)
      local num = ok and aid and aid:match("%d+")
      if num and ABS_ANIM_IDS[num] then absTrigger(model) end
    end)
    cDesc = model.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
      if d:IsA("Sound") then task.defer(absBindSound, d, model, owner, session) end
    end)
    cAnc = model.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
      if not absTarget(model) then owner:Disconnect() end
    end)
    absScanSounds(model, owner, session)
  end

  local function absScanTick()
    local session = absSession
    if not absLive(session) then return end
    local pl = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
    local sv = pl and pl:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
    if absFolder ~= sv then
      absDropTargets()
      absRingsClear()
      absFolder = sv
      if sv then
        local folderToken = {}
        absFolderToken = folderToken
        absFolderConns[#absFolderConns + 1] = sv.ChildAdded:Connect(function(model)
          task.delay(0.15, function()
            if absLive(session) and absFolderToken == folderToken and model.Parent == sv then pcall(absHookModel, model) end
          end)
        end)
        absFolderConns[#absFolderConns + 1] = sv.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(model)
          if absFolderToken ~= folderToken or model.Parent == sv then return end
          local owner = abs.animConns[model]
          if owner then owner:Disconnect() end
          absRingOff(model)
        end)
        absFolderConns[#absFolderConns + 1] = sv.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
          if absFolderToken == folderToken and (sv.Parent ~= workspace:FindFirstChild("Players") or not sv:IsDescendantOf(workspace)) then
            absDropTargets()
            absRingsClear()
          end
        end)
      end
    end
    for model, owner in pairs(abs.animConns) do
      if not absTarget(model) then owner:Disconnect() end
    end
    if sv then
      for _, model in ipairs(sv:GetChildren()) do pcall(absHookModel, model) end
    end
    if not abs.showRing then
      absRingsClear()
    else
      for model, ring in pairs(abs.rings) do
        if not absTarget(model) or not ring.Parent or not players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) then absRingOff(model) end
      end
    end
  end

  local function absStopScan()
    absSession = nil
    if scanThread then pcall(task.cancel, scanThread) scanThread = nil end
    absDropTargets()
    absRingsClear()
    absReleaseTurn()
  end

  local function absStartScan()
    local session = absSession
    if not absLive(session) or scanThread then return end
    local worker
    worker = coroutine.create(function()
      while absLive(session) do
        pcall(absScanTick)
        task.wait(1)
      end
      if scanThread == worker then scanThread = nil end
    end)
    scanThread = worker
    task.spawn(worker)
  end

  local function absSyncRole()
    local ch = localPlayer.Character
    if not abs.on or not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED or not absIsKiller() then
      absStopScan()
      return
    end
    if absSession and absSession.char == ch then absStartScan() return end
    absStopScan()
    absSession = { char = ch }
    absStartScan()
  end

  local function absBindMyChar(ch)
    if abs.myCharConn then pcall(function() abs.myCharConn:Disconnect() end) abs.myCharConn = nil end
    local watch = absWatch
    if ch then
      abs.myCharConn = ch.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if absWatch == watch and localPlayer.Character == ch then
          absStopScan()
          absSyncRole()
        end
      end)
    end
    absSyncRole()
  end

  local function absStart()
    if not abs.on or not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED then return end
    if absWatch then
      if absWatch.ready then absSyncRole() end
      return
    end
    local watch = {}
    absWatch = watch
    absHarvestCatalog()
    if absWatch ~= watch or not abs.on or not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED then return end
    watch.ready = true
    absTrack(localPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Character"):Connect(function()
      if absWatch == watch then absBindMyChar(localPlayer.Character) end
    end))
    absBindMyChar(localPlayer.Character)
  end

  local function absStop()
    abs.on = false
    absStopScan()
    absDropConns()
    pcall(function()
      if abs.gui then abs.gui:Destroy() end
      abs.gui = nil
    end)
  end

  rawset(_G, "NERG_ABS", {
    on = function() return abs.on end,
    ring = function() return abs.showRing end,
    locked = function() return abs.locked end,
    hits = function() return abs.hits end,
    range = function() return abs.range end,
    duration = function() return abs.duration end,
    rings = function()
      local n = 0
      for _ in pairs(abs.rings) do n += 1 end
      return n
    end,
    set = function(v)
      v2.set("a747ABOn", v and true or false)
      if v then abs.on = true absStart() else absStop() end
      return abs.on
    end,
    setRing = function(v)
      abs.showRing = v and true or false
      v2.set("a747ABRing", abs.showRing)
      if not abs.showRing or not abs.on or not absIsKiller() then
        absRingsClear()
      else
