        pcall(absScanTick)
      end
      return abs.showRing
    end,
    setRange = function(n)
      abs.range = math.clamp(tonumber(n) or abs.range, 10, 120)
      v2.set("a747ABRange", abs.range)
      absRingsResize()
      return abs.range
    end,
    setDuration = function(n)
      abs.duration = math.clamp(tonumber(n) or abs.duration, 0.15, 2)
      v2.set("a747ABDur", abs.duration)
      return abs.duration
    end,
    stop = function() absStop() end,
    trigger = function(target) pcall(absTrigger, target) end,
    dbg = function()
      local n = 0
      for _ in pairs(abs.rings) do n += 1 end
      return string.format("on=%s ring=%s killer=%s locked=%s rings=%d range=%.0f dur=%.1f hits=%d model=%s",
        tostring(abs.on), tostring(abs.showRing), tostring(absIsKiller()), tostring(abs.locked), n, abs.range, abs.duration, abs.hits, ABS_MODEL_NAME)
    end,
  })

  -- boot purge: don rac cua lan chay truoc (thu hẹp phạm vi quét nhân vật, xóa bỏ 30.000 objects)
  pcall(function()
    local pf = workspaceService:FindFirstChild("Players")
    local rootsToScan = { pf, localPlayer and localPlayer.Character }
    for _, root in ipairs(rootsToScan) do
      if root then
        for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
          if d:IsA("Part") and d.Name == "NergAbsRing" then d:Destroy() end
        end
      end
    end
    local pg0 = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg0 then
      for _, nm in ipairs({ "NergAbsGui", "AbsGui" }) do
        local old = pg0:FindFirstChild(nm)
        if old then old:Destroy() end
      end
    end
  end)
end

-- [A750] Auto Dodge (Slasher Raging Pace / John Doe 404 Error) — Dynamic Skin Catalog · Zero-Delay Local Animator Mount · Default OFF · Full Cleanup
do
  local autoDodge = {
    on = v2.get("a750DodgeOn", false) and true or false,
    meleeRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(v2.get("a750DodgeMelee", 18)) or 18, 8, 30),
    chanceRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(v2.get("a750DodgeChance", 75)) or 75, 30, 100),
    facingCheck = v2.get("a750DodgeFacing", false) and true or false,
    showNotif = v2.get("a750DodgeNotif", true) and true or false,
    debounce = math.clamp(tonumber(v2.get("a750DodgeCd", 1.0)) or 1.0, 0.5, 3.0),
    lastFired = 0,
    hits = 0,
    conns = {},
    survConns = {},
    gui = nil,
    catalogBuilt = false,
  }

  local STUN_ANIMS = {
    -- Fallback base IDs measured live (docId canon)
    ["87259391926321"] = { char = "Guest1337", skill = "Punch" },
    ["116618003477002"] = { char = "Shedletsky", skill = "Slash" },
    ["100725497418533"] = { char = "TwoTime", skill = "Stab" },
    ["89448354637442"] = { char = "TwoTime", skill = "LungeStart" },
    ["133491532453922"] = { char = "Chance", skill = "AimGun" },
    ["90499469533503"] = { char = "Chance", skill = "GunFire" },
  }

  local STUN_SOUNDS = {
    -- Fallback base sound IDs measured live
    ["133398613783505"] = { char = "Guest1337", skill = "Punch" },
    ["13471740561"] = { char = "Guest1337", skill = "CriticalPunch" },
    ["609342351"] = { char = "Guest1337", skill = "PunchWindup" },
    ["12222208"] = { char = "Shedletsky", skill = "Slash" },
    ["134932463887703"] = { char = "TwoTime", skill = "Stab" },
    ["101489994047160"] = { char = "TwoTime", skill = "CrouchStab" },
    ["139012439429121"] = { char = "Chance", skill = "GunWindUp" },
    ["121120408563206"] = { char = "Chance", skill = "GunFire" },
  }

  local function chanceMisfire(charName, key)
    if charName ~= "Chance" then return false end
    local name = tostring(key):lower()
    return name:find("fail", 1, true) ~= nil or name:find("explode", 1, true) ~= nil
      or name:find("explos", 1, true) ~= nil or name:find("reload", 1, true) ~= nil
      or name:find("click", 1, true) ~= nil
  end

  local function buildDynamicCatalog()
    if autoDodge.catalogBuilt then return end
    autoDodge.catalogBuilt = true
    pcall(function()
      local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
      local assets = rs:FindFirstChild("Assets")
      if not assets then return end

      local function extractStunIds(val, outTbl, infoObj, depth)
        if (depth or 0) > 3 or val == nil then return end
        if type(val) == "string" or type(val) == "number" then
          local num = tostring(val):match("%d+")
          if num then outTbl[num] = infoObj end
        elseif typeof(val) == "Instance" and (val:IsA("Animation") or val:IsA("Sound")) then
          local rawId = val:IsA("Animation") and val.AnimationId or val.SoundId
          local num = tostring(rawId):match("%d+")
          if num then outTbl[num] = infoObj end
        elseif type(val) == "table" then
          for key, subv in pairs(val) do
            if not chanceMisfire(infoObj.char, key) then
              extractStunIds(subv, outTbl, infoObj, (depth or 0) + 1)
            end
          end
        end
      end

      local function harvest(cfgMod, sName)
        if not (cfgMod and cfgMod:IsA("ModuleScript")) then return end
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, cfgMod)
        if not (ok and type(mod) == "table") then return end
        if type(mod.Animations) == "table" then
          for aKey, aVal in pairs(mod.Animations) do
            local ks = tostring(aKey)
            if not chanceMisfire(sName, ks) and (ks:find("Punch") or ks:find("Slash") or ks:find("Stab") or ks:find("Lunge") or ks:find("AimGun") or ks:find("GunFire") or ks:find("Hatchet") or ks:find("Axe")) then
              extractStunIds(aVal, STUN_ANIMS, { char = sName, skill = ks }, 0)
            end
          end
        end
        if type(mod.Sounds) == "table" then
          for sKey, sVal in pairs(mod.Sounds) do
            local ks = tostring(sKey)
            if not chanceMisfire(sName, ks) and (ks:find("Punch") or ks:find("Slash") or ks:find("Stab") or ks:find("Gun") or ks:find("Hatchet") or ks:find("Axe")) then
              extractStunIds(sVal, STUN_SOUNDS, { char = sName, skill = ks }, 0)
            end
          end
        end
      end

      -- Scan base survivors (Guest1337, Shedletsky, TwoTime, Chance, JaneDoe)
      local bSurv = assets:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
      if bSurv then
        for _, sName in ipairs({ "Guest1337", "Shedletsky", "TwoTime", "Chance", "JaneDoe" }) do
          local f = bSurv:FindFirstChild(sName)
          if f then harvest(f:FindFirstChild("Config"), sName) end
        end
      end

      -- Scan all skins
      local sFolder = assets:FindFirstChild("Skins") and assets.Skins:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
      if sFolder then
        for _, sName in ipairs({ "Guest1337", "Shedletsky", "TwoTime", "Chance", "JaneDoe" }) do
          local charSkins = sFolder:FindFirstChild(sName)
          if charSkins then
            for _, sk in ipairs(charSkins:GetChildren()) do
              harvest(sk:FindFirstChild("Config"), sName)
            end
          end
        end
      end    end)
  end

  local function showDodgeBanner(txt)
    if not autoDodge.showNotif then return end
    pcall(function()
      local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
      if not pg then return end
      if not autoDodge.gui or not autoDodge.gui.Parent then
        autoDodge.gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        autoDodge.gui.Name = "NergDodgeGui"
        autoDodge.gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        autoDodge.gui.DisplayOrder = 6
        autoDodge.gui.Parent = pg
      end
      local f = autoDodge.gui:FindFirstChild("Banner")
      if not f then
        f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Name = "Banner"
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 75)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 32)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 25)
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        corner.Parent = f
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Name = "Stroke"
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5
        stroke.Parent = f
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Name = "Text"
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextSize = 13
        lbl.Parent = f
        f.Parent = autoDodge.gui
      end
      local tLbl = f:FindFirstChild("Text")
      if tLbl then tLbl.Text = txt end
      autoDodge.bannerSeq = (autoDodge.bannerSeq or 0) + 1
      local mySeq = autoDodge.bannerSeq
      f.Visible = true
      task.delay(1.8, function()
        if autoDodge.bannerSeq == mySeq and f and f.Parent then f.Visible = false end
      end)
    end)
  end

  local function tryAutoDodge(info, sModel)
    if not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED or not autoDodge.on then return end
    local myChar = localPlayer.Character
    if not (myChar and myChar.Parent and myChar.Parent.Name == "Killers") then return end
    local myHum = myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not (myHum and myHum.Health > 0) then return end

    -- Determine killer ability
    local kBase = tostring(myChar:GetAttribute("CharacterName") or myChar.Name):gsub("^[!#]", "")
    local abilityName, payload = nil, nil
    if kBase == "Slasher" then
      abilityName = "RagingPace"
      payload = buffer.fromstring("\3\n\0\0\0RagingPace")
    elseif kBase == "JohnDoe" or kBase == "JoshDoe" then
      abilityName = "404Error"
      payload = buffer.fromstring("\3\8\0\0\000404Error")
    else
      return
    end

    -- Debounce
    local nowT = os.clock()
    if nowT - autoDodge.lastFired < autoDodge.debounce then return end

    -- Cooldown check via UI
    local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local mu = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("MainUI")
    local ac = mu and mu:FindFirstChild("AbilityContainer")
    local btn = ac and ac:FindFirstChild(abilityName)
    local cdLbl = btn and btn:FindFirstChild("CooldownTime")
    if cdLbl and cdLbl:IsA("TextLabel") then
      local cdTxt = cdLbl.Text
      if cdTxt ~= "" and cdTxt ~= "0" and cdTxt ~= "0.0" then
        local cdVal = tonumber(cdTxt:match("[%d%.]+"))
        if not cdVal or cdVal > 0 then return end
      end
    end

    -- Range check
    local myRoot = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local sRoot = sModel and sModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not (myRoot and sRoot) then return end

    local dist = (myRoot.Position - sRoot.Position).Magnitude
    local isChance = (info.char == "Chance" or (info.skill and info.skill:find("Gun")))
    local maxDist = isChance and autoDodge.chanceRadius or autoDodge.meleeRadius
    if dist > maxDist then return end

    -- Facing check (optional)
    if autoDodge.facingCheck then
      local toKiller = (myRoot.Position - sRoot.Position).Unit
      local look = sRoot.CFrame.LookVector
      if look:Dot(toKiller) < 0.15 then return end
    end

    -- Fire ability
    autoDodge.lastFired = nowT
    autoDodge.hits += 1
    if remoteEvent then
      remoteEvent:FireServer("UseActorAbility", { [1] = payload })
    end

    if v2.get("a720Telemetry", false) then
      print(string.format("[NERG] ★ AutoDodge fired: %s vs %s %s (dist=%.1f)", abilityName, tostring(info.char), tostring(info.skill), dist))
    end
    showDodgeBanner(string.format("★ Auto Dodge: %s vs %s (%s)", abilityName, tostring(info.char), tostring(info.skill)))
  end

  local dodgeSession
  local dodgeScanThread
  local dodgeWatch
  local dodgeCharConn
  local dodgeRoleConn
  local hookedSvFolder
  local dodgeFolderToken
  local dodgeBootPending = true

  local function dodgeIsKiller()
    local ch = localPlayer.Character
    return ch ~= nil and ch.Parent ~= nil and ch.Parent.Name == "Killers" and ch:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
  end

  local function dodgeLive(session)
    return session ~= nil and dodgeSession == session and autoDodge.on
      and __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE and not _G.__NERG_KILLED
      and localPlayer.Character == session.char and dodgeIsKiller()
  end

  local function dodgeTarget(model)
    local pl = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
    local sv = pl and pl:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
    return sv ~= nil and sv:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and model ~= nil
      and model:IsA("Model") and model.Parent == sv
  end

  local function unhookSurvivor(model)
    local conns = autoDodge.survConns[model]
    autoDodge.survConns[model] = nil
    if conns then
      for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    end
  end

  local function hookSurvivor(model)
    local session = dodgeSession
    if not dodgeLive(session) or not dodgeTarget(model) or autoDodge.survConns[model] then return end
    local conns = {}
    autoDodge.survConns[model] = conns
    local function owned()
      return dodgeLive(session) and dodgeTarget(model) and autoDodge.survConns[model] == conns
    end
    local sndConns = {}
    conns[#conns + 1] = {
      Disconnect = function()
        for _, dropFn in pairs(sndConns) do pcall(dropFn) end
      end,
    }
    local function checkSound(d)
      if not owned() or not d:IsDescendantOf(model) then return end
      local num = d.SoundId and tostring(d.SoundId):match("%d+")
      if num and STUN_SOUNDS[num] then tryAutoDodge(STUN_SOUNDS[num], model) end
    end
    local function bindSurvSound(d)
      if not owned() or not (d and d:IsA("Sound") and d:IsDescendantOf(model)) or sndConns[d] then return end
      local cPlay, cPlaying, cId, cAnc
      local function dropSnd()
        sndConns[d] = nil
        if cPlay then pcall(function() cPlay:Disconnect() end) cPlay = nil end
        if cPlaying then pcall(function() cPlaying:Disconnect() end) cPlaying = nil end
        if cId then pcall(function() cId:Disconnect() end) cId = nil end
        if cAnc then pcall(function() cAnc:Disconnect() end) cAnc = nil end
      end
      sndConns[d] = dropSnd
      cPlay = d.Played:Connect(function() checkSound(d) end)
      cPlaying = d:GetPropertyChangedSignal("IsPlaying"):Connect(function()
        if d.IsPlaying then checkSound(d) end
      end)
      cId = d:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SoundId"):Connect(function()
        if d.IsPlaying then checkSound(d) end
      end)
      cAnc = d.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if not d:IsDescendantOf(model) then dropSnd() end
      end)
      if d.IsPlaying then checkSound(d) end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
      if d:IsA("Sound") then bindSurvSound(d) end
    end
    conns[#conns + 1] = model.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
      if d:IsA("Sound") then task.defer(bindSurvSound, d) end
    end)
    conns[#conns + 1] = model.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
      if not dodgeTarget(model) and autoDodge.survConns[model] == conns then unhookSurvivor(model) end
    end)
    local mountThread = coroutine.create(function()
      if not owned() then return end
      local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or model:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
      if not owned() or not (hum and hum.Parent == model) then return end
      local an = hum and (hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5))
      if not owned() or not (an and an.Parent == hum and hum.Parent == model) then return end
      conns[#conns + 1] = an.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
        if not owned() then return end
        local aid = track and track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
        local num = aid and tostring(aid):match("%d+")
        if num and STUN_ANIMS[num] then tryAutoDodge(STUN_ANIMS[num], model) end
      end)
    end)
    conns[#conns + 1] = { Disconnect = function() pcall(task.cancel, mountThread) end }
    task.spawn(mountThread)
  end

  local function dodgeDropTargets()
    dodgeFolderToken = nil
    hookedSvFolder = nil
    for _, key in ipairs({ "svChildAdded", "svChildRemoved", "svAncestry" }) do
      local c = autoDodge.conns[key]
      autoDodge.conns[key] = nil
      if c then pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    end
    for model in pairs(autoDodge.survConns) do unhookSurvivor(model) end
  end

  local function bindDodgeSurvivorsFolder(sv)
    local session = dodgeSession
    if not dodgeLive(session) then return end
    if not (sv and sv.Parent and sv:IsDescendantOf(workspace)) then
      dodgeDropTargets()
      return
    end
    if hookedSvFolder ~= sv then
      dodgeDropTargets()
      hookedSvFolder = sv
      local folderToken = {}
      dodgeFolderToken = folderToken
      autoDodge.conns["svChildAdded"] = sv.ChildAdded:Connect(function(model)
        task.wait(0.1)
        if dodgeLive(session) and dodgeFolderToken == folderToken and model.Parent == sv then pcall(hookSurvivor, model) end
      end)
      autoDodge.conns["svChildRemoved"] = sv.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(model)
        if dodgeFolderToken == folderToken and model.Parent ~= sv then unhookSurvivor(model) end
      end)
      autoDodge.conns["svAncestry"] = sv.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if dodgeFolderToken == folderToken and (sv.Parent ~= workspace:FindFirstChild("Players") or not sv:IsDescendantOf(workspace)) then dodgeDropTargets() end
      end)
    end
    for model in pairs(autoDodge.survConns) do
      if not dodgeTarget(model) then unhookSurvivor(model) end
    end
    for _, model in ipairs(sv:GetChildren()) do pcall(hookSurvivor, model) end
  end

  local function dodgeStopScan()
    dodgeSession = nil
    if dodgeScanThread then pcall(task.cancel, dodgeScanThread) dodgeScanThread = nil end
    dodgeDropTargets()
  end

  local function dodgeSyncRole()
    local ch = localPlayer.Character
    if not autoDodge.on or not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED or not dodgeIsKiller() then
      dodgeStopScan()
      return
    end
    if dodgeSession and dodgeSession.char == ch and dodgeScanThread then return end
    dodgeStopScan()
    local session = { char = ch }
    dodgeSession = session
    local worker
    worker = coroutine.create(function()
      while dodgeLive(session) do
        pcall(function()
          local pl = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
          bindDodgeSurvivorsFolder(pl and pl:FindFirstChild("Survivors"))
        end)
        task.wait(2.0)
      end
      if dodgeScanThread == worker then dodgeScanThread = nil end
    end)
    dodgeScanThread = worker
    task.spawn(worker)
  end

  local function dodgeBindMyChar(ch)
    if dodgeCharConn then pcall(function() dodgeCharConn:Disconnect() end) dodgeCharConn = nil end
    local watch = dodgeWatch
    if ch then
      dodgeCharConn = ch.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if dodgeWatch == watch and localPlayer.Character == ch then
          dodgeStopScan()
          dodgeSyncRole()
        end
      end)
    end
    dodgeSyncRole()
  end

  local function autoDodgeStart()
    if not autoDodge.on or not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED then return end
    if dodgeWatch then
      if dodgeWatch.ready then dodgeSyncRole() end
      return
    end
    local watch = {}
    dodgeWatch = watch
    buildDynamicCatalog()
    if dodgeWatch ~= watch or not autoDodge.on or not __PLUS_GEN_ALIVE or _G.__NERG_KILLED then return end
    watch.ready = true
    dodgeRoleConn = localPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Character"):Connect(function()
      if dodgeWatch == watch then dodgeBindMyChar(localPlayer.Character) end
    end)
    dodgeBindMyChar(localPlayer.Character)
  end

  local function autoDodgeStop()
    dodgeBootPending = false
    dodgeWatch = nil
    dodgeStopScan()
    if dodgeCharConn then pcall(function() dodgeCharConn:Disconnect() end) dodgeCharConn = nil end
    if dodgeRoleConn then pcall(function() dodgeRoleConn:Disconnect() end) dodgeRoleConn = nil end
    for key, c in pairs(autoDodge.conns) do
      pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
      autoDodge.conns[key] = nil
    end
    autoDodge.bannerSeq = (autoDodge.bannerSeq or 0) + 1
    pcall(function()
      if autoDodge.gui then autoDodge.gui:Destroy() autoDodge.gui = nil end
    end)
  end

  rawset(_G, "NERG_DODGE", {
    on = function() return autoDodge.on end,
    hits = function() return autoDodge.hits end,
    melee = function() return autoDodge.meleeRadius end,
    chance = function() return autoDodge.chanceRadius end,
    facing = function() return autoDodge.facingCheck end,
    notif = function() return autoDodge.showNotif end,
    cd = function() return autoDodge.debounce end,
    set = function(v)
      autoDodge.on = v and true or false
      v2.set("a750DodgeOn", autoDodge.on)
      if autoDodge.on then autoDodgeStart() else autoDodgeStop() end
      return autoDodge.on
    end,
    setMelee = function(n)
      autoDodge.meleeRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(n) or autoDodge.meleeRadius, 8, 30)
      v2.set("a750DodgeMelee", autoDodge.meleeRadius)
      return autoDodge.meleeRadius
    end,
    setChance = function(n)
      autoDodge.chanceRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(n) or autoDodge.chanceRadius, 30, 100)
      v2.set("a750DodgeChance", autoDodge.chanceRadius)
      return autoDodge.chanceRadius
    end,
    setFacing = function(v)
      autoDodge.facingCheck = v and true or false
      v2.set("a750DodgeFacing", autoDodge.facingCheck)
      return autoDodge.facingCheck
    end,
    setNotif = function(v)
      autoDodge.showNotif = v and true or false
      v2.set("a750DodgeNotif", autoDodge.showNotif)
      return autoDodge.showNotif
    end,
    setCd = function(n)
      autoDodge.debounce = math.clamp(tonumber(n) or autoDodge.debounce, 0.5, 3.0)
      v2.set("a750DodgeCd", autoDodge.debounce)
      return autoDodge.debounce
    end,
    stop = function() autoDodgeStop() end,
    trigger = function(char, skill)
      local pl = workspace:FindFirstChild("Players")
      local sv = pl and pl:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
      local m = sv and sv:FindFirstChild(char or "Guest1337")
      tryAutoDodge({ char = char or "Guest1337", skill = skill or "Punch" }, m)
    end,
    catalog = function()
      local aN, sN = 0, 0
      for _ in pairs(STUN_ANIMS) do aN += 1 end
      for _ in pairs(STUN_SOUNDS) do sN += 1 end
      return { anims = aN, sounds = sN }
    end,
    status = function()
      local aN, sN = 0, 0
      for _ in pairs(STUN_ANIMS) do aN += 1 end
      for _ in pairs(STUN_SOUNDS) do sN += 1 end
      return string.format("on=%s hits=%d melee=%.0f chance=%.0f facing=%s notif=%s cd=%.1f anims=%d snds=%d",
        tostring(autoDodge.on), autoDodge.hits, autoDodge.meleeRadius, autoDodge.chanceRadius,
        tostring(autoDodge.facingCheck), tostring(autoDodge.showNotif), autoDodge.debounce, aN, sN)
    end,
  })
end

end

do
    local okBatch, batchErr = pcall(TURNsakenLoadKillerBatch1)
    if not okBatch then warn("[TURNsaken] Killers batch 1 failed: " .. tostring(batchErr)) end
    print("[TURNsaken] Killer batch ready: ABS=" .. tostring(_G.NERG_ABS ~= nil) .. " DODGE=" .. tostring(_G.NERG_DODGE ~= nil))
end
TURNsakenLoadKillerBatch1 = nil

(function()
-- ============================================================
-- TURNsaken V4.2 :: reconstructed tail (UI wiring + ported features)
-- ============================================================

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- Shared helpers
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
local function fireRemoteAbility(bufStr)
    if testRemote then
        pcall(function() testRemote:FireServer("UseActorAbility", { buffer.fromstring(bufStr) }) end)
    end
end
local function fireRemoteCharge()
    fireRemoteAbility("\003\006\000\000\000Charge")
end
local function fireRemoteNova()
    fireRemoteAbility("\003\004\000\000\000Nova")
end
local function fireKillerAbility(name)
    if NetworkModule then
        pcall(function() NetworkModule:FireServerConnection("UseActorAbility", "REMOTE_EVENT", name) end)
    end
end
local function isLocalKiller()
    local ch = lp and lp.Character
    return ch ~= nil and ch.Parent ~= nil and ch.Parent.Name == "Killers"
end
local function isLocalSurvivorNow()
    local ch = lp and lp.Character
    return ch ~= nil and ch.Parent ~= nil and ch.Parent.Name == "Survivors"
end
local function nearestKillerRoot(maxDist)
    local ch = lp and lp.Character
    local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not my then return nil end
    local best, bestD = nil, maxDist or math.huge
    for _, k in ipairs(KillersFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if k:IsA("Model") then
            local r = k:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if r then
                local d = (r.Position - my.Position).Magnitude
                if d <= bestD then bestD = d; best = r end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end
local function nearestSurvivorRoot(maxDist)
    local ch = lp and lp.Character
    local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not my then return nil end
    local best, bestD = nil, maxDist or math.huge
    for _, s in ipairs(SurvivorsFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if s:IsA("Model") and s ~= ch then
            local r = s:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if r then
                local d = (r.Position - my.Position).Magnitude
                if d <= bestD then bestD = d; best = r end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end
local function facePosition(root, pos)
    if not root then return end
    local flat = Vector3.new(pos.X - root.Position.X, 0, pos.Z - root.Position.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude > 0.01 then
        root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, Vector3.new(pos.X, root.Position.Y, pos.Z))
    end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- [Charge Aim] Guest1337 冲刺瞄准 (ported from nergsaken)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
local chargeAimOn = false
local autoChargeOn = false
local chargeMaxRange = 20
local chargeAimStep = nil
local chargeAimConn = nil
local chargeAimStart = 0
local lastChargeFire = 0
local GUEST_CHARGE_ANIMS = {}
do
    local added = 0
    local function extract(val, out)
        if type(val) == "string" or type(val) == "number" then
            local n = tostring(val):match("%d+"); if n then out[n] = true end
        elseif typeof(val) == "Instance" and val:IsA("Animation") then
            local n = tostring(val.AnimationId):match("%d+"); if n then out[n] = true end
        elseif type(val) == "table" then
            for _, v in pairs(val) do extract(v, out) end
        end
    end
    local function harvest(cfgMod)
        if not (cfgMod and cfgMod:IsA("ModuleScript")) then return end
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, cfgMod)
        if not (ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.Animations) == "table") then return end
        for k, v in pairs(mod.Animations) do
            local kl = tostring(k):lower()
            if kl:find("charge") or kl:find("dash") then extract(v, GUEST_CHARGE_ANIMS) end
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local a = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
        local s = a and a:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
        local g = s and s:FindFirstChild("Guest1337")
        if g then harvest(g:FindFirstChild("Config")) end
        local sk = a and a:FindFirstChild("Skins")
        local ss = sk and sk:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
        local gs = ss and ss:FindFirstChild("Guest1337")
        if gs then for _, c in ipairs(gs:GetChildren()) do harvest(c:FindFirstChild("Config")) end end
    end)
    for _ in pairs(GUEST_CHARGE_ANIMS) do added = added + 1 end
    print("[TURNsaken] Charge anims loaded: " .. tostring(added))
end

local function chargeReady()
    local pg = lp and lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local mu = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("MainUI")
    local ac = mu and mu:FindFirstChild("AbilityContainer")
    local b = ac and ac:FindFirstChild("Charge")
    local cd = b and b:FindFirstChild("CooldownTime")
    if not cd then return true end
    local t = tostring(cd.Text or "")
    return t == "" or t == "0" or t == "0.0"
end

local function stopChargeAim()
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TURNsakenChargeAim") end)
    if chargeAimConn then pcall(function() chargeAimConn:Disconnect() end); chargeAimConn = nil end
    local ch = lp and lp.Character
    local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.Parent then
        hum.AutoRotate = true
        pcall(function() hum:Move(Vector3.zero) end)
    end
end

local function startChargeAim(targetRoot)
    if not chargeAimOn or not isLocalSurvivorNow() then return end
    local ch = lp.Character
    local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not (my and hum) or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    local target = targetRoot or nearestKillerRoot(chargeMaxRange * 1.5)
    if not target then return end
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GuestCharging") end)
    chargeAimStart = os.clock()
    if hum then hum.AutoRotate = false end
    local function step()
        if not chargeAimOn or os.clock() - chargeAimStart > 1.25 then
            stopChargeAim(); return
        end
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GuestCharging") end)
        if not (ch and ch.Parent and my and my.Parent and hum and hum.Health > 0) then stopChargeAim(); return end
        if not (target and target.Parent) then stopChargeAim(); return end
        local off = target.Position - my.Position
        if off.Magnitude <= 3.5 then stopChargeAim(); return end
        local kVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local flatVel = Vector3.new(kVel.X, 0, kVel.Z)
        local leadT = math.clamp(off.Magnitude / 42, 0.02, 0.18)
        local aimPos = target.Position + flatVel * leadT
        local flat = Vector3.new(aimPos.X - my.Position.X, 0, aimPos.Z - my.Position.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude > 0.01 then
            my.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(my.Position, my.Position + flat.Unit)
            pcall(function() hum:Move(flat.Unit) end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("TURNsakenChargeAim", Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value + 25, step)
    end)
end

local function fireAutoCharge()
    if not autoChargeOn or not chargeReady() or not isLocalSurvivorNow() then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - lastChargeFire < 0.75 then return end
    local ch = lp.Character
    local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local target = nearestKillerRoot(chargeMaxRange)
    if not (my and target) then return end
    if (target.Position - my.Position).Magnitude > chargeMaxRange then return end
    lastChargeFire = now
    fireRemoteCharge()
    if chargeAimOn then startChargeAim(target) end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- [Aimpunch 优化] punch aim steer improvements
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- Existing punch aim (BodyGyro Y-only) is kept; these flags tune it.
local punchAimSmoothVal = 0.6
local punchAimPredictVal = true
-- Enhanced: on punch anim, also snap facing immediately (frame-0 pre-snap)
local function punchPreSnap(root)
    local t = nearestKillerRoot(100)
    if t and root then facePosition(root, t.Position) end
end
task.spawn(function()
    -- Bind a frame-0 pre-snap to the punch listener for a snappier feel.
    local ch = lp.Character
    if not ch then return end
    local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local a = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not a then return end
    a.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(t)
        if not punchAimEnabled then return end
        local aid = t.Animation and tostring(t.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+")
        if aid and PUNCH_ANIM_IDS[aid] then
            local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if punchAimPredictVal then pcall(function() punchPreSnap(root) end) end
        end
    end)
end)

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- [Jane Doe] crystal + hatchet (ported from nergsaken combat ext)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
local jdInstantCrystal = false
local jdCrystalAim = false
local jdHatchetAim = false
local JD_CRYSTAL_CHARGE = { ["106527725058030"] = true }
local JD_CRYSTAL_THROW = { ["139929602101552"] = true }
local JD_HATCHET = { ["111918351126361"] = true }
local jdAimStepConn = nil
local jdAimUntil = 0
local function jdReleaseAim()
    if jdAimStepConn then pcall(function() jdAimStepConn:Disconnect() end); jdAimStepConn = nil end
    jdAimUntil = 0
    local ch = lp.Character
    local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.Parent then hum.AutoRotate = true end
end
local function jdStartAim(duration)
    jdAimUntil = os.clock() + (duration or 0.4)
    if jdAimStepConn then return end
    jdAimStepConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if os.clock() > jdAimUntil or not (jdCrystalAim or jdHatchetAim) then jdReleaseAim(); return end
        local ch = lp.Character
        local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not my then jdReleaseAim(); return end
        local t = nearestKillerRoot(200)
        if t then facePosition(my, t.Position) end
    end)
end
local function jdSyncConfigs()
    -- Instant crystal: zero out charge config when enabled
    pcall(function()
        local a = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
        local s = a and a:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
        local jd = s and s:FindFirstChild("JaneDoe")
        local cfg = jd and require(jd:FindFirstChild("Config"))
        if cfg then
            if jdInstantCrystal then
                if cfg.CrystalChargeTime ~= nil then cfg.CrystalChargeTime = 0 end
                if cfg.CrystalChargeDuration ~= nil then cfg.CrystalChargeDuration = 0 end
            end
        end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    local ch = lp.Character
    if not ch then return end
    local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local a = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not a then return end
    a.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(t)
        if not (jdCrystalAim or jdHatchetAim) then return end
        if not (ch and ch.Parent and ch.Parent.Name == "Survivors" and ch.Name:find("JaneDoe")) then return end
        local aid = t.Animation and tostring(t.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+")
        if aid and (JD_CRYSTAL_THROW[aid] or JD_CRYSTAL_CHARGE[aid]) and jdCrystalAim then
            jdStartAim(0.5)
        elseif aid and JD_HATCHET[aid] and jdHatchetAim then
            jdStartAim(0.6)
        end
    end)
end)
lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5); jdSyncConfigs() end)

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- [Veeronica] Sk8 suite (ported from nergsaken combat ext)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
local veeTrickStam = false
local veeWallRebound = false
local veeAutoPhase = false
local veePhaseLast = 0
local VEE_SK8_ANIMS = { ["123803922491274"] = true, ["117058860640843"] = true }
local function fireVeeTrick()
    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
    task.wait(0.02)
    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.25)
        if veeTrickStam and SprintModule then
            local ch = lp.Character
            if ch and ch.Name == "Veeronica" then
                pcall(function()
                    SprintModule.Stamina = math.min(SprintModule.MaxStamina or 100, (SprintModule.Stamina or 0) + 10)
                end)
            end
        end
        if veeAutoPhase and isLocalSurvivorNow() then
            local ch = lp.Character
            if ch and ch.Name == "Veeronica" and os.clock() - veePhaseLast > 1.5 then
                -- Phase when a killer is close (safety boost)
                local t = nearestKillerRoot(12)
                if t then
                    veePhaseLast = os.clock()
                    fireKillerAbility("Phase") -- fire via actor ability
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        if not veeWallRebound then continue end
        local ch = lp.Character
        if not (ch and ch.Parent and ch.Name == "Veeronica") then continue end
        local my = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not (my and hum) then continue end
        -- Simple wall rebound: raycast ahead, steer away if close
        local look = my.CFrame.LookVector
        local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude < 0.01 then continue end
        flat = flat.Unit
        local hit = Workspace:Raycast(my.Position, flat * 3.5)
        if hit then
            local n = hit.Normal
            local t1 = Vector3.new(-n.Z, 0, n.X)
            local t2 = -t1
            local d1 = flat:Dot(t1); local d2 = flat:Dot(t2)
            local steer = (d1 >= d2) and t1 or t2
            pcall(function() hum:Move(steer) end)
        end
    end
end)

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- [Sprint Management] (ported from nergsaken stamCore.mgmt)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
local sprintMgmtOn = false
local sprintMgmtPct = 10
local sprintMgmtConn = nil
local sprintMgmtLocked = false
local function sprintMgmtStop()
    if sprintMgmtConn then pcall(function() sprintMgmtConn:Disconnect() end); sprintMgmtConn = nil end
    sprintMgmtLocked = false
    pcall(function() if SprintModule then SprintModule.BindsEnabled = true end end)
end
local function sprintMgmtStart()
    if sprintMgmtConn then return end
    sprintMgmtConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not sprintMgmtOn then sprintMgmtStop(); return end
        if not SprintModule then return end
        if not (SprintModule.MaxStamina and SprintModule.MaxStamina > 0) then return end
        local below = ((SprintModule.Stamina or 0) / SprintModule.MaxStamina) * 100 < sprintMgmtPct
        if below then
            sprintMgmtLocked = true
            pcall(function() SprintModule.BindsEnabled = false end)
            if SprintModule.IsSprinting == true then
                pcall(function() SprintModule.IsSprinting = false end)
                pcall(function() if SprintModule.Toggle then SprintModule:Toggle(false) end end)
            end
        elseif sprintMgmtLocked then
            sprintMgmtLocked = false
            pcall(function() SprintModule.BindsEnabled = true end)
        end
    end)
end


-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- [Killers] M1 Aim + Noli + Nosferatu + Azure (ported)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
local killerM1Aim = false
local killerM1AimConn = nil
local killerM1AimUntil = 0
local KILLER_M1_ANIMS = {}
do
    local function extract(val, out)
        if type(val) == "string" or type(val) == "number" then
            local n = tostring(val):match("%d+"); if n then out[n] = true end
        elseif typeof(val) == "Instance" and val:IsA("Animation") then
            local n = tostring(val.AnimationId):match("%d+"); if n then out[n] = true end
        elseif type(val) == "table" then
            for _, v in pairs(val) do extract(v, out) end
        end
    end
    local order = { "Attack", "Slash", "SlashAir", "EnragedSlash", "Bite", "Stab", "SlashGround", "GolemSlash", "Swing" }
    local function harvest(cfgMod)
        if not (cfgMod and cfgMod:IsA("ModuleScript")) then return end
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, cfgMod)
        if not (ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.Animations) == "table") then return end
        for _, role in ipairs(order) do
            local v = mod.Animations[role]
            if v then extract(v, KILLER_M1_ANIMS) end
        end
        for k, v in pairs(mod.Animations) do
            local kl = tostring(k):lower()
            if kl:find("slash") or kl:find("swing") or kl:find("stab") or kl:find("bite") or kl:find("attack") then
                extract(v, KILLER_M1_ANIMS)
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local a = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
        local ks = a and a:FindFirstChild("Killers")
        if ks then for _, k in ipairs(ks:GetChildren()) do harvest(k:FindFirstChild("Config")) end end
    end)
end
local function killerM1AimRelease()
    if killerM1AimConn then pcall(function() killerM1AimConn:Disconnect() end); killerM1AimConn = nil end
    killerM1AimUntil = 0
    local ch = lp.Character
    local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.Parent then hum.AutoRotate = true end
end
local function killerM1AimStart(duration)
    killerM1AimUntil = os.clock() + (duration or 0.35)
    if killerM1AimConn then return end
    killerM1AimConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if os.clock() > killerM1AimUntil or not killerM1Aim then killerM1AimRelease(); return end
        if not isLocalKiller() then killerM1AimRelease(); return end
        local ch = lp.Character
        local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not my then killerM1AimRelease(); return end
        local t = nearestSurvivorRoot(60)
        if t then facePosition(my, t.Position) end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    local function hookChar()
        local ch = lp.Character
        if not ch then return end
        local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local a = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not a then return end
        a.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(t)
            if not killerM1Aim or not isLocalKiller() then return end
            local aid = t.Animation and tostring(t.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+")
            if aid and KILLER_M1_ANIMS[aid] then killerM1AimStart(0.35) end
        end)
    end
    hookChar()
    lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5); hookChar() end)
end)

-- ----- Noli (Void Rush) -----
local noliRushCtrl = false
local noliRushAim = false
local noliSpeedOn = false
local noliDashSpeed = 60
local noliTurnStr = 6
local noliAntiCrash = false
local noliAutoNova = false
local noliNovaRadius = 12
local noliAimStepConn = nil
local noliLastNova = 0
local function isLocalNoli()
    local ch = lp.Character
    return ch ~= nil and (ch.Name == "Noli" or ch:GetAttribute("CharacterName") == "Noli") and ch.Parent == KillersFolder
end
local function noliSyncConfig()
    pcall(function()
        local a = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
        local ks = a and a:FindFirstChild("Killers")
        local n = ks and ks:FindFirstChild("Noli")
        local cfg = n and require(n:FindFirstChild("Config"))
        if cfg then
            if noliSpeedOn and cfg.VoidRushDashSpeed ~= nil then cfg.VoidRushDashSpeed = noliDashSpeed end
            if noliRushCtrl and cfg.VoidRushTurnSpeed ~= nil then cfg.VoidRushTurnSpeed = noliTurnStr end
        end
    end)
end
local function noliUnbindAim()
    if noliAimStepConn then pcall(function() noliAimStepConn:Disconnect() end); noliAimStepConn = nil end
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TURNsakenNoliAim") end)
end
local function noliBindAim()
    if noliAimStepConn then return end
    if not noliRushAim then return end
    noliAimStepConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local ch = lp.Character
        if not (isLocalNoli() and ch) then noliUnbindAim(); return end
        local st = ch:GetAttribute("VoidRushState")
        if st ~= "Dashing" then return end
        local my = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not my then return end
        local t = nearestSurvivorRoot(200)
        if not t then return end
        local flat = Vector3.new(t.Position.X - my.Position.X, 0, t.Position.Z - my.Position.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude > 0.01 then
            my.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(my.Position, my.Position + flat.Unit)
        end
    end)
end
local function noliUnbindAntiCrash()
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TURNsakenNoliAntiCrash") end)
end
local function noliBindAntiCrash()
    if not noliAntiCrash then noliUnbindAntiCrash(); return end
    pcall(function()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("TURNsakenNoliAntiCrash", Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value + 1, function()
            local ch = lp.Character
            if not (isLocalNoli() and ch) then return end
            local st = ch:GetAttribute("VoidRushState")
            if st ~= "Dashing" then return end
            local my = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not my then return end
            local look = my.CFrame.LookVector
            local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
            flat = flat.Unit
            local hit = Workspace:Raycast(my.Position, flat * 2.5)
            if hit then
                local n = hit.Normal
                local t1 = Vector3.new(-n.Z, 0, n.X); local t2 = -t1
                local steer = (flat:Dot(t1) >= flat:Dot(t2)) and t1 or t2
                my.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(steer.X * noliDashSpeed, my.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, steer.Z * noliDashSpeed)
            end
        end)
    end)
end
local function noliSyncState()
    noliSyncConfig()
    if noliRushAim then noliBindAim() else noliUnbindAim() end
    if noliAntiCrash then noliBindAntiCrash() else noliUnbindAntiCrash() end
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.4)
        if isLocalNoli() and (noliRushCtrl or noliSpeedOn) then noliSyncConfig() end
    end
end)
-- Auto Nova: detect Nova projectile near survivors, detonate
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.2)
        if not noliAutoNova or not isLocalNoli() then continue end
        if os.clock() - noliLastNova < 0.6 then continue end
        pcall(function()
            local pr = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Systems") and ReplicatedStorage.Systems:FindFirstChild("Player")
                and ReplicatedStorage.Systems.Player:FindFirstChild("Game")
                and ReplicatedStorage.Systems.Player.Game:FindFirstChild("ProjectileReplication")
            if not pr then return end
            local projMod = require(pr)
            local live = projMod and projMod.Projectiles
            if type(live) ~= "table" then return end
            local sf = SurvivorsFolder
            for id, p in pairs(live) do
                local mdl = p and p.Model
                if typeof(mdl) == "Instance" and mdl.Parent then
                    if mdl.Name == "Voidstar" or mdl.Name:find("Voidstar") or mdl.Name:find("Nova") then
                        local pPos
                        pcall(function() pPos = mdl:IsA("BasePart") and mdl.Position or mdl:GetPivot().Position end)
                        if pPos then
                            for _, s in ipairs(sf:GetChildren()) do
                                local sh = s:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local hum = s:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if sh and hum and hum.Health > 0 and (sh.Position - pPos).Magnitude <= noliNovaRadius then
                                    noliLastNova = os.clock()
                                    fireRemoteNova()
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

-- ----- Nosferatu + Azure (QTE + aim + turn control) -----
local nosAutoQTE = false
local nosQteDelay = 0.085
local nosBatsAim = false
local nosSpireCtrl = false
local nosSpireTurn = 25
local nosAscentCtrl = false
local nosAscentAim = false
local azAutoQTE = false
local azQteDelay = 0.12
local azGrabAim = false
local azNoGrabStam = false
local qteConn = nil
local qteActive = false
local function isLocalNosferatu()
    local ch = lp.Character
    return ch ~= nil and (ch.Name == "Nosferatu" or ch:GetAttribute("CharacterName") == "Nosferatu") and ch.Parent == KillersFolder
end
local function isLocalAzure()
    local ch = lp.Character
    return ch ~= nil and (ch.Name == "Azure" or ch:GetAttribute("CharacterName") == "Azure") and ch.Parent == KillersFolder
end
-- Auto QTE: send a virtual key press for the active QTE prompt
local function stepAutoQTE(delay)
    pcall(function()
        local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        local tu = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("TemporaryUI")
        local q = tu and tu:FindFirstChild("QTE")
        if not q then return end
        -- Press a WASD key / Space based on QTE zone presence (generic: press E / Space)
        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
        task.wait(delay)
        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
    end)
end
local function nosAzSync()
    -- Sync configs for turn control
    pcall(function()
        local a = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
        local ks = a and a:FindFirstChild("Killers")
        if nosSpireCtrl or nosAscentCtrl then
            local n = ks and ks:FindFirstChild("Nosferatu")
            local cfg = n and require(n:FindFirstChild("Config"))
            if cfg then
                if nosSpireCtrl and cfg.CataclysmTurnSpeed ~= nil then cfg.CataclysmTurnSpeed = nosSpireTurn end
                if nosAscentCtrl and cfg.DiveTurnSpeed ~= nil then cfg.DiveTurnSpeed = nosSpireTurn end
            end
        end
    end)
    -- Manage QTE heartbeat
    if (nosAutoQTE or azAutoQTE) and not qteConn then
        qteConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not (nosAutoQTE or azAutoQTE) then if qteConn then qteConn:Disconnect() qteConn = nil end return end
            if qteActive then return end
            qteActive = true
            task.spawn(function()
                local d = nosAutoQTE and nosQteDelay or azQteDelay
                stepAutoQTE(d)
                task.wait(0.05)
                qteActive = false
            end)
        end)
    elseif not (nosAutoQTE or azAutoQTE) and qteConn then
        qteConn:Disconnect(); qteConn = nil
    end
end
-- Grab aim (Azure) / dive aim (Nosferatu): turn toward nearest survivor while relevant
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        if (nosBatsAim or nosAscentAim) and isLocalNosferatu() then
            local ch = lp.Character
            local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local t = my and nearestSurvivorRoot(200)
            if my and t then facePosition(my, t.Position) end
        elseif azGrabAim and isLocalAzure() then
            local ch = lp.Character
            local my = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local t = my and nearestSurvivorRoot(200)
            if my and t then facePosition(my, t.Position) end
        end
    end
end)
-- No grab stamina penalty (Azure)
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.4)
        if azNoGrabStam and isLocalAzure() and SprintModule then
            pcall(function() SprintModule.StaminaLoss = 0 end)
        end
    end
end)


-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- [ESP] extended: Player Tags + Objects + Map Items + Projectiles
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
espEnabled.Tags = false
espEnabled.Objects = false
espEnabled.ObjItems = false
espEnabled.ObjTags = false
espEnabled.AzureRadius = false
espEnabled.MapItems = false
espEnabled.MapDist = false
espEnabled.Projectiles = false
espEnabled.Windup = false
espColors.Objects = Color3.fromRGB(255, 192, 203)
espColors.ObjItems = Color3.fromRGB(89, 0, 255)
espColors.AzureTrap = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30)
espColors.Projectile = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)

local espTagOptions = {
    Name = true, Role = true, Health = true, Distance = true,
    Stamina = true, Velocity = true, Device = true, Abilities = true,
}
local function tagTextFor(m)
    local parts = {}
    if espTagOptions.Name then
        local nm = m:GetAttribute("Username") or m.Name
        parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(nm)
    end
    if espTagOptions.Role then
        local r = m:GetAttribute("CharacterName") or ""
        if r ~= "" then parts[#parts + 1] = "[" .. tostring(r) .. "]" end
    end
    local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local r = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if espTagOptions.Health and h then parts[#parts + 1] = "HP " .. math.floor(h.Health) end
    if espTagOptions.Distance and r and lp.Character then
        local my = lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if my then parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("%dm", math.floor((r.Position - my.Position).Magnitude)) end
    end
    if espTagOptions.Stamina and h then
        pcall(function()
            if SprintModule and type(SprintModule.Stamina) == "number" and type(SprintModule.MaxStamina) == "number" then
                parts[#parts + 1] = "ST " .. math.floor(SprintModule.Stamina / SprintModule.MaxStamina * 100) .. "%"
            end
        end)
    end
    if espTagOptions.Velocity and r then
        local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("V %.0f", Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z).Magnitude)
    end
    if espTagOptions.Device then
        local d = m:GetAttribute("Device") or "PC"
        parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(d)
    end
    return table.concat(parts, "  |  ")
end

-- Self-contained object ESP (own cache, independent of the main espCache)
local objEspCache = {}
local function objRemoveESP(m)
    local d = objEspCache[m]
    if d then
        pcall(function() if d.hl then d.hl:Destroy() end end)
        pcall(function() if d.bb then d.bb:Destroy() end end)
        objEspCache[m] = nil
    end
end
local function objCreateESP(m, color, textFunc)
    if not m or not m.Parent then return nil end
    local cached = objEspCache[m]
    if cached then
        if cached.hl then cached.hl.FillColor = color; cached.hl.OutlineColor = color end
        if cached.lbl and textFunc then pcall(function() cached.lbl.Text = textFunc() end) end
        return cached
    end
    local ok, d = pcall(function()
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Name = "TURNsaken_ObjHL"
        hl.Adornee = m
        hl.FillColor = color; hl.OutlineColor = color
        hl.FillTransparency = 0.55; hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Parent = m
        local root = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m:FindFirstChild("Main") or m.PrimaryPart
            or (m:IsA("BasePart") and m) or m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        local bb, lbl = nil, nil
        if root and textFunc then
            bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "TURNsaken_ObjBB"; bb.Adornee = root
            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 24); bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true; bb.Parent = root
            lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0); lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.TextColor3 = color; lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0); lbl.TextSize = 11; lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            lbl.Text = textFunc() or ""
        end
        return { hl = hl, bb = bb, lbl = lbl, color = color }
    end)
    if ok and d then objEspCache[m] = d; return d end
    return nil
end

local objFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
objFolder.Name = "TURNsakenObjESP"
objFolder.Parent = Workspace

local projLinePool = {}
local projLineUsed = 0
local function getProjLine(i)
    local p = projLinePool[i]
    if not p then
        p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanQuery = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CastShadow = false
        p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        p.Parent = objFolder
        projLinePool[i] = p
    end
    return p
end
local function hideProjLines()
    for i = 1, projLineUsed do projLinePool[i].Transparency = 1 end
    projLineUsed = 0
end
local function placeProjLine(a, b, color)
    local mag = (b - a).Magnitude
    projLineUsed = projLineUsed + 1
    local p = getProjLine(projLineUsed)
    if mag < 0.05 then p.Transparency = 1; return end
    p.Size = Vector3.new(0.12, 0.12, mag)
    p.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((a + b) / 2, b)
    p.Color = color
    p.Transparency = 0.2
end

local azureRadiusPart = nil
local function drawAzureRadius()
    if not espEnabled.AzureRadius then
        if azureRadiusPart then azureRadiusPart:Destroy(); azureRadiusPart = nil end
        return
    end
    local target = nil
    for _, k in ipairs(KillersFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if k:IsA("Model") and (k.Name == "Azure" or k:GetAttribute("CharacterName") == "Azure") then target = k end
    end
    if not target then
        if azureRadiusPart then azureRadiusPart:Destroy(); azureRadiusPart = nil end
        return
    end
    local r = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not r then return end
    if not azureRadiusPart then
        azureRadiusPart = Instance.new("Part")
        azureRadiusPart.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        azureRadiusPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 24, 24)
        azureRadiusPart.Color = espColors.AzureTrap
        azureRadiusPart.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
        azureRadiusPart.Transparency = 0.6
        azureRadiusPart.Anchored = false; azureRadiusPart.CanCollide = false; azureRadiusPart.CanTouch = false; azureRadiusPart.CanQuery = false; azureRadiusPart.CastShadow = false; azureRadiusPart.Massless = true
        azureRadiusPart.Parent = r
        local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint"); w.Part0 = r; w.Part1 = azureRadiusPart; w.Parent = azureRadiusPart
    end
end

local function scanObjects(am)
    local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local roots = {}
    if map then roots[#roots + 1] = map end
    roots[#roots + 1] = Workspace
    local seen = {}
    for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
        for _, o in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if seen[o] then continue end
            seen[o] = true
            if not (o:IsA("Model") or o:IsA("BasePart")) then continue end
            local n = o.Name:lower()
            local kind = nil
            if n:find("graffiti") or n:find("spray") then kind = "Graffiti"
            elseif n:find("taph") or n:find("trap") then kind = "Taph"
            elseif n:find("ritual") then kind = "Ritual"
            elseif n:find("builderman") then kind = "Builderman"
            elseif n:find("azure") then kind = "Azure"
            elseif espEnabled.ObjItems and (n:find("medkit") or n:find("bloxy") or n:find("cola") or n:find("gear")) then kind = "Item"
            end
            if kind then
                local model = o:IsA("Model") and o or o:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or o
                local col = (kind == "Item") and espColors.ObjItems or espColors.Objects
                am[model] = { color = col, text = espEnabled.ObjTags and (kind .. " (" .. o.Name .. ")") or nil }
            end
        end
    end
end

local mapItemNames = { graffiti = true, spray = true, fruit = true, fruits = true, candy = true, pumpkin = true }
local function scanMapItems(am)
    local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if not map then return end
    for _, o in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
        if o:IsA("Model") or o:IsA("BasePart") then
            local n = o.Name:lower()
            local kind = nil
            for k in pairs(mapItemNames) do
                if n:find(k) then kind = k; break end
            end
            if kind then
                local model = o:IsA("Model") and o or o:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or o
                am[model] = {
                    color = espColors.Item,
                    text = function()
                        local pos = model:IsA("BasePart") and model.Position or model:GetPivot().Position
                        local my = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if espEnabled.MapDist and my then return o.Name .. " " .. string.format("%dm", math.floor((pos - my.Position).Magnitude)) end
                        return o.Name
                    end,
                }
            end
        end
    end
end

local function scanProjectiles()
    hideProjLines()
    if not espEnabled.Projectiles then return end
    pcall(function()
        local pr = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Systems") and ReplicatedStorage.Systems:FindFirstChild("Player")
            and ReplicatedStorage.Systems.Player:FindFirstChild("Game")
            and ReplicatedStorage.Systems.Player.Game:FindFirstChild("ProjectileReplication")
        if not pr then return end
        local mod = require(pr)
        local live = mod and mod.Projectiles
        if type(live) ~= "table" then return end
        for _, p in pairs(live) do
            local mdl = p and p.Model
            if typeof(mdl) == "Instance" and mdl.Parent then
                local pos
                pcall(function() pos = mdl:IsA("BasePart") and mdl.Position or mdl:GetPivot().Position end)
                if pos then
                    local vel = (type(p.Velocity) == "Vector3") and p.Velocity or (mdl:IsA("BasePart") and mdl.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero)
                    placeProjLine(pos, pos + vel * 0.5, espColors.Projectile)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Object / map-item / azure / projectile apply loop
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.7)
        drawAzureRadius()
        scanProjectiles()
        local am = {}
        if espEnabled.Objects or espEnabled.ObjItems then scanObjects(am) end
        if espEnabled.MapItems then scanMapItems(am) end
        for m, i in pairs(am) do
            local d = objCreateESP(m, i.color, i.text)
            if d and d.hl then
                d.hl.FillColor = i.color; d.hl.OutlineColor = i.color
            end
            if d and d.lbl and i.text then
                pcall(function() d.lbl.Text = i.text end)
            end
        end
        for m in pairs(objEspCache) do
            if not am[m] then objRemoveESP(m) end
        end
    end
end)

-- Player tags: enrich killer/survivor ESP labels
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.4)
        if not espEnabled.Tags then continue end
        for m, d in pairs(espCache) do
            if d.lbl and m.Parent and (m.Parent == KillersFolder or m.Parent == SurvivorsFolder) then
                pcall(function() d.lbl.Text = tagTextFor(m) end)
            end
        end
    end
end)

            frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 250)
            frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 12, 24)
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame.Visible = previewOn and true or false
            frame.Parent = gui
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10); c.Parent = frame
            local s = Instance.new("UIStroke"); s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180); s.Thickness = 1.5; s.Transparency = 0.2; s.Parent = frame

            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22)
            title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 4)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            title.TextSize = 12
            title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.Text = "3D HITBOX PREVIEW (3D碰撞箱)"
            title.Parent = frame

            local vpf = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
            vpf.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -32)
            vpf.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 28)
            vpf.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 6, 12)
            vpf.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
            vpf.BorderSizePixel = 0
            vpf.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 220)
            vpf.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 250)
            vpf.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1.2, -1)
            vpf.Parent = frame
            local vc = Instance.new("UICorner"); vc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8); vc.Parent = vpf

            local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
            cam.FieldOfView = 50
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(12, 9, -12), Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
            cam.Parent = vpf
            vpf.CurrentCamera = cam

            local wm = Instance.new("WorldModel")
            wm.Parent = vpf

            local floor = Instance.new("Part")
            floor.Anchored = true; floor.CanCollide = false; floor.CanQuery = false; floor.CastShadow = false
            floor.Size = Vector3.new(40, 0.2, 40)
            floor.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 22, 34)
            floor.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            floor.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -0.1, 0)
            floor.Parent = wm

            local box = Instance.new("Part")
            box.Anchored = true; box.CanCollide = false; box.CanQuery = false; box.CastShadow = false
            box.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40)
            box.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
            box.Transparency = 0.5
            box.Size = Vector3.new(4.5, 6, 7.5)
            box.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
            box.Parent = wm

            local edges = {}
            for i = 1, 12 do
                local p = Instance.new("Part")
                p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanQuery = false; p.CastShadow = false
                p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                p.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
                p.Parent = wm
                edges[i] = p
            end

            local function updatePreview()
                local base = TURNsakenBaseHitboxSize or Vector3.new(4.5, 6, 7.5)
                local sc = TURNsakenHitboxScale or 1.6
                local ws = TURNsakenHitboxWidthScale or 1.0
                local size = Vector3.new(math.max(0.5, base.X * sc * ws), math.max(0.5, base.Y * sc), math.max(0.5, base.Z * sc))
                box.Size = size
                box.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, size.Y * 0.5, 0)
                local hx, hy, hz = size.X * 0.5, size.Y * 0.5, size.Z * 0.5
                local cf = CFrame.new(0, hy, 0)
                local corners = {
                    cf * Vector3.new(-hx, -hy, -hz), cf * Vector3.new( hx, -hy, -hz),
                    cf * Vector3.new( hx, -hy,  hz), cf * Vector3.new(-hx, -hy,  hz),
                    cf * Vector3.new(-hx,  hy, -hz), cf * Vector3.new( hx,  hy, -hz),
                    cf * Vector3.new( hx,  hy,  hz), cf * Vector3.new(-hx,  hy,  hz),
                }
                local idx = { {1,2},{2,3},{3,4},{4,1},{5,6},{6,7},{7,8},{8,5},{1,5},{2,6},{3,7},{4,8} }
                for i, pr in ipairs(idx) do
                    local a = corners[pr[1]]
                    local b = corners[pr[2]]
                    local mag = (b - a).Magnitude
                    local e = edges[i]
                    e.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, mag)
                    e.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((a + b) * 0.5, b)
                end
            end
            updatePreview()

            setPreviewVisible = function(on)
                frame.Visible = on and true or false
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                while gui.Parent do
                    if frame.Visible then pcall(updatePreview) end
                    task.wait(0.25)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end

-- ---------------- HDT ----------------
do
    local b = TabHDT:AddLeftGroupbox("碰撞箱拖拽 (HDT)", "move")
    hitboxDraggingTech = CfgGet("hdt_enabled", false)
    b:AddToggle("hdt_enabled", {Text = "Enable HDT (启用拖拽)", Default = hitboxDraggingTech, Callback = function(v) hitboxDraggingTech = v; CfgSet("hdt_enabled", v) end})

    b:AddToggle("sv_chargeaim", {Text = "Charge Aim (冲刺瞄准)", Default = chargeAimOn, Callback = function(v) chargeAimOn = v; if not v then stopChargeAim() end; CfgSet("sv_chargeaim", v) end})
    autoChargeOn = CfgGet("sv_autocharge", false)
    b:AddToggle("sv_autocharge", {Text = "Auto Charge (自动冲刺)", Default = autoChargeOn, Callback = function(v) autoChargeOn = v; CfgSet("sv_autocharge", v) end})
    chargeMaxRange = CfgGet("sv_chargerange", chargeMaxRange)
    b:AddSlider("sv_chargerange", {Text = "Charge Max Range", Min = 10, Max = 35, Rounding = 0, Default = chargeMaxRange, Callback = function(v) chargeMaxRange = v; CfgSet("sv_chargerange", v) end})
    local b2 = TabSurvivor:AddLeftGroupbox("Jane Doe (珍)", "snowflake")
    jdInstantCrystal = CfgGet("sv_jd_instant", false)
    b2:AddToggle("sv_jd_instant", {Text = "Instant Crystal Charge", Default = jdInstantCrystal, Callback = function(v) jdInstantCrystal = v; jdSyncConfigs(); CfgSet("sv_jd_instant", v) end})
    jdCrystalAim = CfgGet("sv_jd_crystalaim", false)
    b2:AddToggle("sv_jd_crystalaim", {Text = "Crystal Silent Aim", Default = jdCrystalAim, Callback = function(v) jdCrystalAim = v; CfgSet("sv_jd_crystalaim", v) end})
    jdHatchetAim = CfgGet("sv_jd_hatchet", false)
    b2:AddToggle("sv_jd_hatchet", {Text = "Hatchet Aim", Default = jdHatchetAim, Callback = function(v) jdHatchetAim = v; CfgSet("sv_jd_hatchet", v) end})
    local b3 = TabSurvivor:AddLeftGroupbox("Veeronica (维罗妮卡)", "skateboard")
    veeronicaAutoTrick = CfgGet("sv_vee_trick", false)
    b3:AddToggle("sv_vee_trick", {Text = "Auto Trick (自动花式)", Default = veeronicaAutoTrick, Callback = function(v) veeronicaAutoTrick = v; CfgSet("sv_vee_trick", v) end})
    veeronicaControl = CfgGet("sv_vee_ctrl", false)
    b3:AddToggle("sv_vee_ctrl", {Text = "Sk8 Turn Control (滑板转向)", Default = veeronicaControl, Callback = function(v) veeronicaControl = v; CfgSet("sv_vee_ctrl", v) end})
    veeTrickStam = CfgGet("sv_vee_trickstam", false)
    b3:AddToggle("sv_vee_trickstam", {Text = "Trick Stamina Boost", Default = veeTrickStam, Callback = function(v) veeTrickStam = v; CfgSet("sv_vee_trickstam", v) end})
    veeWallRebound = CfgGet("sv_vee_rebound", false)
    b3:AddToggle("sv_vee_rebound", {Text = "Wall Rebound (防撞墙)", Default = veeWallRebound, Callback = function(v) veeWallRebound = v; CfgSet("sv_vee_rebound", v) end})
    veeAutoPhase = CfgGet("sv_vee_phase", false)
    b3:AddToggle("sv_vee_phase", {Text = "Auto Phase Boost", Default = veeAutoPhase, Callback = function(v) veeAutoPhase = v; CfgSet("sv_vee_phase", v) end})
    local b4 = TabSurvivor:AddLeftGroupbox("其它", "eye")
    stunSpy = CfgGet("sv_stunspy", false)
    b4:AddToggle("sv_stunspy", {Text = "Stun Spy (眩晕提示)", Default = stunSpy, Callback = function(v) stunSpy = v; CfgSet("sv_stunspy", v) end})
